```
    locations/                    full CRUD, inline edit (LocationManager); hours and map pin are editable since 2026-10-09/10 (section 28 "Branch opening hours"; existing values are
                                  preserved via coalesce) — a follow-up if per-location hours editing is needed; invalidates the cache
    settings/                     restaurants.features + restaurants.settings, one Card+<form> per section (Features, Tax, Service fee, Ordering,
                                  Payments, Reservations, Delivery, Loyalty, Receipt); reservations.tables (per-table inventory) left out, same
                                  reasoning as locations' hours; invalidates the cache
    payments/                     view-only transaction list (listPayments already joins order_number/customer_name); no actions.ts
    reports/                     Sales Reports (section 22): summary cards, trend charts, CSV export; reports/export/route.ts
    orders/activity/route.ts     polled by the sound-notifications watcher (section 22); not a page
  error.tsx                     renders error.message directly (AppError messages are already user-safe)
```

**UI/UX pass (2026-09-30, every page, at the requester's direction).** Functionality, permissions, business logic and the database were not touched — this was presentation only, built on the existing theme tokens (section 19). New shared components in `components/admin/`, all drop-in replacements for markup every page used to repeat on its own:
- `admin-page-header.tsx` (`AdminPageHeader`): title + description + optional back-link/badge/actions. Every list, detail and settings page now uses it instead of its own `<h1 className="text-[1.75rem]...">` — which also quietly fixes two pages (`menu/items/new`, `menu/items/[itemId]`) that had drifted to a different size than the rest. Title size dropped 1.75rem -> 1.375rem, and the dashboard's "Service" hero 2.2/2.8rem -> 1.8/2.15rem: a back-office screen reads better when the title doesn't out-weigh the data below it (explicit part of the brief — "avoid oversized headings").
- `admin-status-tabs.tsx` (`AdminStatusTabs`): the pill filter row (orders/menu-category/payments/reservations/reviews all had byte-identical copies of this).
- `admin-pagination.tsx` (`AdminPagination`): the Previous/Next pager (orders/customers/payments/reviews/reservations, same story).
- `admin-empty-state.tsx` (`AdminEmptyState`): every "No X match this filter" was a bare `<p>`; now a small icon + text, denser than the storefront's `EmptyState` (no serif heading, no accent ring — this is a back-office screen, not a moment to sell something).
- `confirm-dialog.tsx` (`useConfirm()` hook): replaces every `window.confirm(...)` (7 files: category/coupon/delivery-zone/location/addon-group/variant managers, `item-row-actions`) with a themed, promise-based dialog on `@radix-ui/react-alert-dialog` (already an installed, previously-unused dependency). Usage: `const { confirm, dialog } = useConfirm(); if (!(await confirm({ title, variant: "danger", confirmLabel: "Delete" }))) return; ...` and render `{dialog}` once in the component. New keyframes `dialog-pop`/`overlay-fade` in `globals.css`, same `@keyframes` + `@utility` convention as `animate-sheet` — covered by the file's existing global reduced-motion block, no new one needed. Never scales from 0 (starts at `scale(0.95)`), matches the "nothing in the real world appears from nothing" rule.
- `admin-skeleton.tsx` (`AdminHeaderSkeleton`/`AdminTabsSkeleton`/`AdminTableSkeleton`/`AdminStatSkeleton`/`AdminCardListSkeleton`): building blocks for `loading.tsx`. **Every route segment under `(dashboard)/` now has its own `loading.tsx`** shaped like that page (table skeleton for orders/customers/payments/reservations/menu, card-list for reviews, form skeleton for settings/menu item pages, kitchen's card grid) — deliberately one per segment rather than relying on the parent's, because Next nests Suspense boundaries: a leaf route with no `loading.tsx` of its own falls back to the nearest ancestor's, which would show the *dashboard's* Service-pass skeleton while navigating to, say, Settings.
- Confirmation dialogs and skeletons are pure UI states with no server round trip of their own — nothing to test with vitest; verified by screenshot (see below) and by typecheck/build.
- **Responsive strategy, unchanged and deliberate:** admin tables scroll horizontally on phones (`overflow-x-auto` on a `min-w-[42rem]` table, already the existing pattern) rather than reflowing into per-row cards — a standard, accepted SaaS pattern, and the sidebar already swaps for `AdminMobileNav`'s drawer below `lg`. Verified at 375/834/1280px with Playwright (`chromium.launch({ channel: "chrome" })` when Playwright's own browser isn't downloaded) against a second `next dev` on port 3100 / `NEXT_DIST_DIR=.next-verify`, signed in with a locally-minted `rp_admin_session` cookie (`signStaffSession`, scoped with `path: adminPath(slug)` — the cookie is set with that path in `web/session.ts`, so a test client must set it the same way) — no horizontal page-level overflow at any width.

- **Auth:** `web/session.ts#signInStaffSession` (sets `rp_staff_session`) / `signOutStaffSession` / `requireStaffForAdmin` (redirects to `adminPath("/login")` = `/admin/login` on `UNAUTHORIZED` instead of throwing, mirroring `requireStorefront`'s `notFound()` pattern). Page-level gating uses `requirePermission(...)`; the sidebar filters links with `hasAnyPermission` (`server/auth/permissions.ts`) — hiding a link is UX only, `requirePermission` in the page/action is the real gate. Some screens have `.view` and `.manage` split across different roles (e.g. `manager` gets `locations.view` but not `locations.manage`) — those pages take a `canManage` flag and hide write controls, not just gate the action (`locations/page.tsx` → `LocationManager`, `settings/page.tsx`'s `readOnly` prop are the reference).
- **Admin login layout:** `/admin/login` reuses the storefront `AuthShell`, whose `lg:min-h` is capped at 46rem because on the storefront a header and footer surround it. On the header-less admin login, the page wrapper is `grid min-h-dvh` so the shell stretches to the full viewport. Without that, a blank strip shows under the cover photo.
- **Seeded logins:** `owner@bellanapoli.pk` / `BellaNapoli#1` (owner), `admin@…` / `BellaNapoli#2`, `manager@…` / `BellaNapoli#3`, `chef@…` / `BellaNapoli#4` (staff role — no `menu.manage`/`staff.manage`, good for testing permission gating).
- **Brand theme:** the admin shell is themed like the storefront, not a fixed color scheme — `web/admin.ts#getAdminTheme` (cached per request) resolves `websites.theme` JSONB (via `server/services/restaurants.ts#getAdminTheme`, → `repositories/websites.ts#getWebsite` → `server/domain/storefront-context.ts#resolveTheme`), falling back to the `restaurants.primary_color` column when no website theme is set — the same resolution the storefront uses, minus the "is this restaurant publicly visible" gate (staff must see their branding even before the site goes live). `(dashboard)/layout.tsx` applies it with `web/theme.ts#themeCssVariables` on the shell's inline `style`, exactly like `src/app/(site)/layout.tsx` does.
- **Admin order flow performance (2026-10-04).** Measured before (local `next start`, hosted pooler, ~0.35 s per round trip): orders list 17 round trips / ~3.1 s, order detail 18 / ~5 s, kitchen 15 (+2 per active ticket) / ~4.5 s, the 7 s order-activity poll 10 / ~4.4 s; one status click ≈ 13–15 s (staff check + 6-statement update + the action's re-render of the page + a second full render from `router.refresh()`). What changed, and the rules that keep it fast:
  - **No `router.refresh()` after an admin server action that calls `revalidatePath`/sets a cookie** — Next already answers such an action with the current page re-rendered (same lesson as section 16's OTP fix). `OrderStatusControl` lost its refresh; `updateOrderStatusAction` revalidates `/orders`, `/orders/<n>`, `/kitchen` and the dashboard, so whichever is open is the one re-rendered in the response.
  - **Order reads are ONE statement:** `repositories/orders.ts` `ORDER_ITEMS_JSON` (items with nested add-ons as one JSON column) and `ORDER_RELATIONS_JSON` (+ history, latest payment, delivery) are used by `getOrderByNumber`, `getOrderById`, `getOrderForAccessGrant` (the customer tracking page benefits too) and `listKitchenOrders` (no per-ticket queries any more — it used to be `1 + 2×tickets`). Mapped by `itemsFromJson`/`applyOrderRelations` with the SAME mappers: they accept JSON numbers/ISO strings as well as pg rows (`money()`, `iso()`, `num()`); verified old-vs-new identical on every order of the dev DB.
  - **`updateOrderStatus` is one statement** (`returning id, order_number, status`) and returns `OrderStatusChange`, not the whole order; the triggers do history/notification/NOTIFY/delivery/stats in the same transaction. A `note` adds a second statement (the trigger-inserted history row is only visible to a later statement — a CTE cannot see it). DB test `checkout.test.ts` now reads history via `getOrderById`.
  - **`listOrders`**: page + total in one statement (`count(*) over ()`); only a page past the end falls back to a count query.
  - **Staff check** (`auth-service.ts#authenticateStaff`): one statement (membership + the restaurant it is checked against) and a 30 s per-process cache (`server/cache/ttl.ts`) for page views and GET routes. **Server actions always re-read** (`web/session.ts#isServerActionRequest` — the `Next-Action` header; every admin write is a server action, the admin route handlers are GET-only — keep it that way or pass `fresh` yourself). Team writes (`services/team.ts`) call `invalidateStaffActors()`. Trade-off: a deactivated member can still VIEW pages on another instance for ≤30 s; they cannot change anything.
  - **Admin restaurant + theme** (`web/admin.ts` → `services/restaurants.ts#getAdminRestaurantContext` → `repositories/restaurants.ts#getRestaurantWithThemeBySlug`): one statement for both (was two transactions in a row, the theme via `select * from websites`), cached 30 s per process, cleared by `invalidateAdminRestaurants()` in every restaurant write (`updateRestaurantFeatures`/`updateRestaurantSettingsSection`). Display only: writes read the row themselves (`requireCurrentRestaurant`), and the **settings page uses `getAdminRestaurantFresh`** because its forms save what they show. `requireRestaurant` (storefront writes) is untouched and still always reads the database (`tests/storefront-services.test.ts`).
  - Order pages start their order queries alongside the restaurant lookup using `actor.restaurantId` (the staff check proved the session belongs to that slug's restaurant); the activity poll and kitchen no longer read the restaurant row at all.
  - Tests: `tests/admin-orders.test.ts` (statement budgets, JSON mapping, list totals, status change, staff/restaurant caching incl. fresh + invalidation + per-slug keys, ttl cache, no refresh after the action).
  - Not done (follow-up): one live stream per restaurant on the existing `notify_order_change` trigger to replace the 7 s poll and the 20 s kitchen refresh.
- **Reservation flow performance (2026-10-04, optimized 2026-10-11).** Originally: a booking click = the action's fresh restaurant read (3 round trips) + a separate account read (3) + the booking transaction (5–7) + `revalidatePath` re-rendering the whole `/reservation` page (7) ≈ 18–20 round trips. First pass (2026-10-04) removed `revalidatePath` and combined reads. Final optimization (2026-10-11): slots are generated from cached location hours (no DB query for availability), and table assignment is removed (restaurant accommodates unlimited parties).
  - **`bookReservation`** (`repositories/reservations.ts`): ONE transaction, ONE read — restaurant (status, time zone, features, settings), the location (hours, active), the customer's row `for update` — then validation and one insert: **3 round trips** (+1 only when the account has no mobile yet: `customers.ts#saveFirstAccountPhone`, shared with `createOrder`). No query for booked slots or table availability; the advisory lock on (location, date) prevents race conditions. Everything is read fresh in the transaction; the action routes with `requireStorefrontRestaurant` (snapshot) only to find the restaurant id. The read is privileged, re-applying RLS: non-`active` restaurant is NOT_FOUND. Account's saved mobile/email win over the form's.
  - **`createReservation`** (still used by admin/API): same advisory lock, same ONE read pattern, one insert. No table assignment (null); staff manage seating manually or via a dedicated UI.
  - **Slot generation:** `/reservation` page generates all slots from location hours (`location.hours[dayKey]`) + `slotMinutes` intervals, cached in the storefront snapshot — **zero DB query**. Multi-branch times are used automatically (each branch has its own `hours`). All slots displayed as available (capacity constraints are gone, restaurant accommodates any party size). Removed `getBookedSlotCounts` call and `booked` slot filtering; removed `pickTable`/table assignment functions.
  - **Validation rulebook:** `assertBookable` (enabled → party size → valid/future/inside advance window → open at that time) is shared by both functions, ensuring consistency. Removed the table-capacity part (unused since tables are not assigned).
  - **No `revalidatePath` in `bookTableAction`:** form shows confirmation from the result; "Book another" calls `router.refresh()` to pick up the slot just taken. Page is dynamic, so any visit reads availability fresh.
  - `/reservation` page: profile read only (when signed in), runs with `Promise.all`. Admin list (`listReservations`) returns rows + total in one statement.
  - ~~Known gap: two customers booking the same slot at the same instant.~~ **Fixed 2026-10-09:** `repositories/reservations.ts#lockBookingDay` takes `pg_advisory_xact_lock` on (branch, date) BEFORE the read (+1 round trip). `tests/reservation-concurrency.test.ts`: six guests, same instant → correctly blocked.
  - Tests: `tests/reservation-booking.test.ts`.
- **Whole-project query pass (2026-10-04) — what was measured, what changed, and the rules.**
  - **Trimming `select *` to the mapped columns is NOT where the time is.** Measured per table (real data, `pg_column_size`): the mappers already read nearly every column, so the unread part is 8–64 B/row — not worth churn. The exception was `customers`: `CUSTOMER_COLUMNS` was literally `*`, so every customer read (lists, profile, checkout, bookings, Google lookup) carried `password_hash` + `google_sub` (~210 of ~340 B/row, and secrets). It is now exactly what `mapCustomer` reads; sign-in selects `password_hash` explicitly where it checks it. `attachAccountCustomer`/`saveFirstAccountPhone` return those columns too.
  - **The real waste was loading whole objects to use one number:** the storefront layout ran `getMyOrders` (active + up to 20 past orders, full rows, every item: 3 statements, ~5.6 KB on average per page view, far more for regular customers) on EVERY signed-in page view only to show `current.length`. Now `countActiveVisitorOrders` (one `count(*)`, ~0.04 KB). `/current-orders` asks `getMyOrders(…, { history: false })` (`historyLimit: 0`). **Rule: when a page shows a count, read a count.**
  - Live order stream (`openOrderStream`): `findVisitorOrder(…, { withDetails: false })` — the order row only, twice per opened tracking page (it was the full order with items/history/payment/delivery, ~4.5 KB each). `getOrderForAccessGrant` takes the same option.
  - Notification dispatcher: the order context reads its items in the same statement (`ORDER_ITEMS_JSON`/`itemsFromJson`, exported from `orders.ts`) — it was two more queries per event.
  - **Menu reorder** (`reorderMenuItems`/`reorderCategories`): ONE `update … from unnest($1::uuid[], $2::int[])` (it was one UPDATE per row — a 7-item reorder took 4–7.5 s, now ~0.35–0.9 s). An id listed twice keeps its last position, as the old loop did.
  - Lists `listCustomers`/`listPayments`/`listReviews` (like orders/reservations): rows + total in one statement (`count(*) over ()`; only a page past the end falls back to a count). Sales report (`getSalesAnalytics`): the four breakdowns are one statement (sub-selects with the same SQL; money sums cast to text inside the JSON so they keep the exact decimal strings). Admin menu page and customer detail run their independent reads with `Promise.all`.
  - **Measured and deliberately left alone:** the snapshot menu query's per-item subqueries (`SUMMARY_COLUMNS`, popularity) run in ~1 ms server time on the indexed tables — rewriting them would be risk for nothing; the snapshot loads in the background anyway.
  - Verified old-vs-new on the dev DB for every restaurant (copies of the old repositories; writes rolled back): customer reads/lists/lookups, the active-order count vs the old full load, current orders, the lean access-grant read, the notification context, the sales report over several ranges/time zones, payments and reviews lists — all identical except one pre-existing ordering tie (below). Reorder checked inside rolled-back transactions: identical positions. Tests: `tests/query-budget.test.ts`.
  - **Order line positions (migration `0028_order_line_positions.sql`, 2026-10-04).** An order's lines (and a line's add-ons) are inserted in one statement, so they share `created_at` (35 of 43 dev orders) and "order by created_at" was a tie — line order could change between queries/refreshes. `order_items.position` / `order_item_addons.position` (int, default 0) = place in the tray / within the line, written by `createOrder` and by every seed script; every reader sorts `position, created_at` (`ORDER_ITEMS_JSON`, `getItemsForOrders`, `buildReorderLines`, the lists' `item_preview`). The migration numbers existing rows by (created_at, physical order): verified in a rolled-back transaction that all 43 dev orders keep the exact line order they showed before. Additive: code without the column still inserts (default 0). **Deploy order: apply 0028 BEFORE running code from this change** — `createOrder` inserts `position`, so against a database without the column placing an order fails. Note the shared hosted DB also has a `0027_branch_scoping.sql` from a teammate that is not in this checkout yet (hence 0028). Verification script pattern: apply the migration inside `begin … rollback` on the migrator connection and run the app's own SQL against it.
- **Orders:** `server/services/orders.ts` adds thin staff-facing wrappers (`listOrdersForStaff`, `getOrderForStaff`, `changeOrderStatus`, `getOrderStatusCounts`, `getRecentOrdersForAdmin`, `getKitchenOrders`) over the already-complete `repositories/orders.ts`. `updateOrderStatusAction` calls `dispatchDueNotifications` inside `after()` after the status commits — every status change must go through this action (also reused by Kitchen), or pushes/emails stay queued.
- **Menu:** `server/services/menu-admin.ts` wraps the already-complete CRUD in `repositories/menu.ts` (categories, items, variants, add-on groups, add-ons) and calls `getStorefrontCache().invalidate()` on every write. Variant/add-on-group/add-on rows are each their own immediate add/delete action (no batch diff/save) — simpler and matches the thin-action convention. `createMenuItem`'s `dietary_tags`/`allergens` insert and `createDeliveryZone`'s `areas`/`postal_codes` insert both needed an explicit `::text[]` cast added to their `coalesce(...)` (found when each was exercised here for the first time) — Postgres otherwise resolves an untyped `coalesce($param, '{}')` as `text`, not the array column's real type; the fix is the same one-line pattern in both files.
- **Settings:** `server/validation/settings.ts` derives every admin edit schema from `shared/contract/settings.ts`'s `restaurantFeaturesSchema`/`restaurantSettingsSchema` (via `.partial()` / `.shape.<section>.removeDefault().partial()`) — never hand-duplicate that field list. `restaurants.features`/`restaurants.settings` are whole-column JSONB (`repositories/restaurants.ts#updateRestaurant` replaces the column, it does not merge per key), so `server/services/restaurants.ts#updateRestaurantFeatures`/`updateRestaurantSettingsSection` always read the current restaurant first and merge the patch into it before writing back — skipping that merge silently wipes every other section.
- **Forms:** native `<form>` + `useTransition` + `FormData`/plain-object payloads, matching `checkout-form.tsx` (no `react-hook-form`, despite it being an installed dependency). Forms with nested data (`MenuItemForm`, coupon/delivery-zone/settings forms) pass a plain object instead of `FormData`; validated server-side regardless.
- **`auth` schema gotcha (see section 6):** staff sign-in was the first code path in this app ever exercised against a real hosted Supabase database for a privileged write, and `authenticateStaff` (which every admin page load calls, via `requireStaff`) was the first RLS-checked read on `team_members` in production. Both failed with `permission denied for schema auth` — the first because `signInStaff` wrote `auth.users.last_sign_in_at` (removed, it was non-essential bookkeeping nothing read), the second because RLS policies call `app.current_user_id()` → `auth.uid()` and `app_runtime` had no real schema `USAGE` on `auth` (Supabase silently no-ops a direct `grant usage on schema auth to app_runtime`, even though 0006 already tried). Fixed with three migrations: `0017` (`app_service` gets table-level `select` on `auth.users`), `0018` and `0019` (`app_service` and `app_runtime` each get membership in Supabase's `service_role`, the one thing that does grant real schema `USAGE` on `auth`). **Updated 2026-09-25:** customer sign-up no longer touches `auth.users` at all (section 16, `0021_customers_own_login.sql`) — that was the "not fixed" gap this line used to describe. Staff creation still can't INSERT/UPDATE `auth.users` through the app (confirmed nothing can — see section 16's incident note) and goes through `scripts/db/create-staff.ts` (migrator connection) instead; that's accepted as the permanent design, not a pending fix.

## 18. Extra demo restaurants (Zaytoun, Blue Hour, Villa, Bun Intended) and per-restaurant seeds

**Zaytoun — Levantine Kitchen** (`zaytoun`, Islamabad, olive/saffron theme, 7 categories / 35 items, 2 locations, 5 delivery zones, 4 coupons, 10 orders, 9 reviews, 6 reservations). Everything restaurant-specific lives in one folder:

```
scripts/db/zaytoun/images/   logo.svg, hero/cover/about.jpg, gallery/*.jpg, menu/*.jpg   (source of truth for the pictures)
scripts/db/zaytoun/seed/     data.ts (all content, ids, prices)  seed.ts (entry point)  images.json + fetch-images.mjs (Unsplash ids -> files)
```

- **Run:** `npm run db:seed:zaytoun`. It replaces **only** the `zaytoun` tenant (delete by slug + its 4 `auth.users`; cascades), so it is re-runnable and never touches Bella/Sakura; `npm run db:seed` (Bella) is unchanged and does not create Zaytoun. Uses the owner connection like the Bella seed. Orders are priced with the real `calculatePricing`; the seed then deletes `notification_events` for this restaurant only (seeded orders are history, nothing may be emailed).
- **Images:** Next only serves `/public`, so the seed copies `scripts/db/zaytoun/images` to `public/images/zaytoun` (URLs in the DB are `/images/zaytoun/...`; the copy is committed so deploys work). Missing pictures: `node scripts/db/zaytoun/seed/fetch-images.mjs` (idempotent, Unsplash direct links; `images.json` maps file -> photo id; menu 1000x750, hero/cover 1920x1080, gallery 1600x1067). Changing a picture in place: delete the file and its `public/images/zaytoun` copy, re-fetch, and clear `.next/cache/images` (the optimizer caches by URL). Choose photos by *looking* at them (no alcohol/bottles in a halal restaurant; a first hero had a vodka bottle).
- **`media.path` is globally unique (`unique (bucket, path)`)**: the Zaytoun seed stores `zaytoun/<file>`; a bare `dining-room.jpg` collides with Bella's.
- **Viewing it:** the storefront cache serves one restaurant per instance (section 15), so run `NEXT_PUBLIC_DEFAULT_RESTAURANT=zaytoun npx next dev -p 3100` (a shell env var beats `.env.local`) and open `/` (the URL never names the restaurant; the env var picks it). Admin: `owner@zaytoun.pk` / `temp/123` (`admin@`, `manager@`, `chef@`, same domain and password — reset 2026-09-27 via `npm run db:create-staff -- --email <addr> --name "<name>" --role <role> --password "temp/123" --restaurant zaytoun`; the seed data's original `Zaytoun#1..4` are stale now, only true for a fresh `npm run db:seed` re-run); customer logins `noor.ahmed@example.com` and `mehr.jamil@example.com` / `DinerPass#1`.
- **Adding another restaurant:** copy `scripts/db/zaytoun/` to `scripts/db/<slug>/`, rewrite `data.ts` (fresh uuid group prefix in `uid()` so ids never clash), point `seed.ts` and a new npm script at it. Only `Playfair Display` and `Inter` are actually loaded (`app/layout.tsx`); other font names fall back to Georgia/system. Icon names must exist in `components/storefront/icon.tsx`. Variant/add-on names used by seeded orders must match exactly (the seed throws on a typo).
- **Blue Hour Coffee — cafe / coffee-shop tenant (`blue-hour`, Lahore, added 2026-09-27).** Same layout: `scripts/db/blue-hour/{images,seed}`, run `npm run db:seed:blue-hour` (replaces only `blue-hour`; uid prefix `b1e0`). 7 categories / 36 items (espresso bar, cold coffee, slow bar, tea & matcha, bakery, desserts, all-day breakfast) with cup-size variants (`Small · 8 oz` / `Regular · 12 oz` / `Large · 16 oz`), a required Milk group and syrup/shot extras; 2 cafés, 4 zones, 4 coupons, 10 orders, 9 reviews, 6 reservations. Theme "Navy & Porcelain": primary `#14305E`, white background/surface, night `#0B1B36`, accent cornflower `#4C86F0` (3.5:1 on white = icons/stars only, 4.9:1 on night) with `accentForeground` = night navy (a white badge label on that accent is under 4.5:1), fonts DM Serif Display + DM Sans, header `tone: "light"`, service fee off. No UI code was needed: the theme tokens and sections carry it. Admin `owner@bluehour.coffee` / `BlueHour#1` (`admin@` #2, `manager@` #3, `barista@` #4); customers `ayesha.khan@example.com`, `fatima.noor@example.com` / `DinerPass#1`. Its `seed.ts` is the Zaytoun one with the tenant strings moved to `data.ts` (`RESTAURANT.city`, `SHIFT_LEAD`, per-customer `postalCode`, location keys `gulberg|dha`). **Dry-run any seed without persisting:** patch `pg.Client.prototype.query` so the first call opens an outer `begin`, inner `begin`/`commit` become no-ops and `end()` issues `rollback`, then `await import("./seed.ts")` (verified this way on 2026-09-27 against the hosted DB, ~2.5 min).
- **Villa The Grand Buffet — real-brand buffet tenant (`villa-grand-buffet`, Lahore, added 2026-09-27).** `scripts/db/villa-grand-buffet/{images,seed}`, run `npm run db:seed:villa` (replaces only its own slug; uid prefix `d11a`; `seed.ts` is the Blue Hour one with location keys `gulberg|lakeCity`). Brand facts from public listings only: 67 MM Alam Road Gulberg III (+92 311 1777241) and Canal Commercial Block M-3A Lake City (+92 311 1777480), 12:45–23:00 daily, 90-minute seatings, Pakistani/Continental/Pan-Asian/Middle Eastern with live BBQ/tandoor/sushi/hot-pot/dessert stations, kids 3–7 half price, "One Buffet. Many Worlds.". Palette sampled from the official YouTube avatar (navy ground `#0F2451`, gold line art `~#C6A15B`): primary `#13295B`, ivory background `#FBF8F1`, night `#0C1A3F`, accent antique gold `#A8823A` (deeper than the logo gold so it still clears 3:1 on ivory; 4.8:1 on night; badge text = night), Cormorant Garamond + Manrope, header `tone: "dark"`. The logo SVG is an original line-art villa + VILLA / THE GRAND BUFFET lockup, not the official file; photos are Unsplash stand-ins. The menu is "Villa at home" (8 categories / 35 takeaway dishes); the per-head buffet itself is booked through reservations (`slotMinutes 15`, `defaultDurationMinutes 90`, `autoConfirm false`) and explained in the home/about content, never sold as a cart item. No official website/email was found: `RESTAURANT.email` and staff logins use the reserved `villagrandbuffet.example` domain (`owner@` / `VillaGrand#1`, `admin@` #2, `manager@` #3, `kitchen@` #4); customers (`zainab.farooq@example.com`, `maryam.ali@example.com` / `DinerPass#1`), orders, reviews and reservations are demo data to delete before going live.
- **Bun Intended — fictional Gen-Z smash-burger brand (`bun-intended`, Karachi, added 2026-09-27).** `scripts/db/bun-intended/{images,seed}`, run `npm run db:seed:bun-intended` (own slug only; uid prefix `b0b5`; `seed.ts` = the Blue Hour one with location keys `clifton|dha`). 12 categories / 39 items: signatures, smash, classics, crispy chicken, Hot Zone, veggie, loaded fries & sides, wings & nuggets, sauces, shakes, drinks, combos. Theme "Grape Soda": primary electric grape `#5B2EFF` (white text 6.4:1), accent hot orange `#F25116` (3.2:1 on cream = icons/stars/badge fill only; badge text black 5.3:1), night `#121212`, cream `#FFF4E0`, Poppins + DM Sans, radius `xl`, header dark, open 13:00–03:00 (04:00 Fri/Sat; overnight windows are supported by `shared/hours`). Reservations off (`features.reservations` + `settings.reservations.enabled` false, `RESERVATIONS = []`, nav has no booking link). **What the seed can and cannot express with today's components:** "Build your burger" = the item `build-your-own-burger` with required Bun/Patty/Cheese/Sauce groups + a 6-pick Toppings group, linked from a `cta` section; fries/drinks rows = `menu_preview` with `itemSlugs`; combos = `featured_items` whose items carry `compareAtPrice`, which is the only card badge (`Offer`) besides spice flames; UGC = the `gallery` section titled `#BunIntended`. Popular/New stickers, rotated badges, thick borders and offset shadows need component changes (`dish-tile.tsx` badge logic, a per-theme style token), not seed data. Admin `owner@bunintended.pk` / `BunIntended#1`; customers `inaya.rizvi@example.com`, `hoorain.naqvi@example.com` / `DinerPass#1`.
- **GRUK — DEMO tenant of a REAL Lahore restaurant, built to pitch the platform to it (`gruk`, added 2026-10-20).** `scripts/db/gruk/{images,seed}`, run `npm run db:seed:gruk` (replaces only `gruk`; uid prefix `6a0c`; `seed.ts` = the Bun Intended one cut to ONE branch, location key `cantt`, pages home/about/contact/menu/locations). **Real, from public sources:** Instagram @grukitup (name, "Highkey Worth it", "Burgers. Tenders. Fries.", Falcon Complex, Bagh Ali Road, Cantt, phone 0323 2133339, "Get Gruk'd", "We don't do medium.", maroon/cream look) and its foodpanda menu (7 categories / 19 items, names, descriptions, list prices — the undiscounted price, not foodpanda's 10% app deal; "(For reference only)" dropped; Tenders show "from Rs 850" there, sizes unknown, seeded as one price). GRUK's own Add Ons (cheese 150, patty 250, gherkins 100) are an optional group on every burger and its Dips (150 each) on tenders/fries, so "Add to Cart" stays one tap. **Stand-ins (say so when pitching, replace before going live):** photos copied from `bun-intended/images` (stock), `menu-banner.jpg` = `cover.jpg` cropped 1920×640 with `sharp`, the REAL roundel logo (`logo.png`, cropped from the Instagram profile picture, ~190px source: fine for the header, ask the owner for the original file), hours 13:00–01:00 (02:00 Fri/Sat), an approximate pin, two delivery zones/fees, coupons GETGRUKD/HIGHKEY, `gruk.example` email/staff, 5 example.com customers and 8 orders (admin demo). The 5 items without a photo (3 add-ons, water, Pepsi) use ORIGINAL flat illustrations generated with `sharp` (`images/menu/{extra-cheese-slice,single-patty,gherkins-add-on,aquafina-water,pepsi}.jpg`; not photos, no brand logos): no web images were downloaded because Google Images only returns watermarked / licensed stock and the shell cannot reach most hosts. Existing DBs get them with `update menu_items set image_url = '/images/gruk/menu/' || slug || '.jpg' where slug in (...) and restaurant_id = (select id from restaurants where slug = 'gruk')`. **No reviews** (a real restaurant's demo must not show invented reviews; `features.reviews` off), except the OPTIONAL `scripts/db/gruk/reviews.ts` (10 written SAMPLE reviews, additive, `--remove` deletes them; run only on request, never present them to GRUK as real, remove before going live, and the reviews feature/section still has to be turned on), no reservations, no dine-in (a takeaway spot). Templates (v3, after the owner pointed at Blue Hour as the benchmark: v1 maroon/Poppins and v2 dark/Anton were both rejected): LIGHT, airy and Blue-Hour-like: theme "Get Gruk'd — paper & maroon" (paper ground `#FFFBF5`, white cards, near-black type `#1A1112`, maroon `#7B1E26` buttons, mustard `#E8B04B` accent, black `#150C0D` bands for hero / closing CTA / footer, `Anton` headings (the condensed poster face of their posts, the owner asked for it explicitly; headlines are written in CAPS in the data) + DM Sans, radius lg, `dark: false`), CAPS poster headlines, default hero (height lg, overlay 0.5, three highlights), default `order_type_switch`, a `menu_categories` photo-tile section, featured sections with their own `eyebrow`, About lead kept to one short line (the lead renders in the display face), header `style: "centered"`, tone light (delivery + pickup only), menu layout `cards`, dock home/menu/locations/account, tax 16% INCLUDED (menu prices are what customers pay), COD + cash. Checked: every section/theme/settings value parses unchanged through the real zod schemas, every demo-order dish/add-on/coupon exists, and a full dry run against the hosted DB (pg patched to one outer transaction + rollback) completed in ~26 s and left 0 `gruk` rows. Admin `owner@gruk.example` / `GrukDemo#1` (`manager@` #2, `kitchen@` #3). **Platform pieces added for it (useful to any tenant):** every flexible section pins its own ground with `background` (`auto` | `paper` | `muted` | `night`, default `auto` = the alternating rhythm of `section-rhythm.ts`): featured_items, menu_categories, menu_preview, about, gallery, why_choose_us, reviews, locations, contact, rich_text. Schema `backgroundSchema` (`shared/contract/sections.ts`), `fixedTone` honours it, each renderer takes `tone ?? <its old default>`, super-admin editor shows "Background". NOT on hero / announcement / stats_band / reservation_cta / page_content (photo-driven) nor cta / order_type_switch (they keep their own `tone` option); dish detail + customizer prices are compact like the menu; the rich-text drop cap only applies to a lede longer than 140 chars; the admin column wash is deliberately faint (brand 6% / accent 9%); a restaurant can also pin the admin page background to a flat colour instead of that automatic wash (`theme.adminBackground`, optional hex; super-admin → Website → Colours → "Admin portal"); theme.ts then emits `--color-admin-wash` + `--admin-wash-image: none`, which `.admin-column` in globals.css reads ahead of the gradient — blank (default) keeps the automatic wash; the menu banner (`menu-hero.tsx`) carries `data-hero-overlay` + `-mt-[var(--header-h)]`, so on `/menu` the header floats transparent over the banner and turns solid on scroll exactly like a home hero (header logic: `site-header.tsx`, `HERO_SELECTOR`); menu dish grids size their columns to the dish count (`dishGridCols` in `(site)/menu/page.tsx`: 1-3 dishes never leave empty columns; list/cards layout likewise) and the sticky category rail (`station-nav.tsx`) centres itself (`safe center`, so an overflowing rail still scrolls from its start) instead of spreading few categories edge to edge; Anton in `src/web/fonts.ts` + `FONT_STACKS`; a single-weight heading face (`SINGLE_WEIGHT_HEADINGS` in `theme.ts`) emits `--display-synthesis: none` (no faux bold; `.theme-root` reads it) and `--display-tracking: 0.012em` (globals.css h1–h4 and `.display-*` use `var(--display-tracking, <old negative value>)`, so serif/sans tenants are unchanged — negative tracking crushes a condensed face); `featured_items.eyebrow` (optional, default "Signature dishes", editable in super-admin); menu prices on dish tile / list item / product card use `formatMoney(..., compact: true)` ("Rs 1,040", not ".00"); the product-card price tag text is `--color-ink` (the old brand/ink mix went muddy brown on dark themes).
- **Bug found and fixed while doing this: per-restaurant colours never applied.** `globals.css` declares `--color-brand: var(--brand-primary, #c8102e)` (and the other `--color-*`, `--radius-brand`) on `:root`; a `var()` in a custom property is resolved where it is declared, so the wrapper's `--brand-*` override never reached components using `bg-[var(--color-brand)]`. Every restaurant rendered the default red (invisible until now because Bella's own brand is that red; `@theme inline` does not help because components reference the variable directly). Fix: `web/theme.ts#themeCssVariables` also emits `--color-brand|-foreground|-secondary|-accent`, `--color-canvas|surface|ink|muted-ink|hairline` and `--radius-brand` on the wrapper (storefront layout and admin shell both use it). Any new token in `globals.css` that reads a `--brand-*` must be added there too.
- **Website pages other than home (`/about`, `/contact`, ...)** are served by `app/(site)/[pageSlug]/page.tsx` (found 2026-09-26: the pages were seeded and `is_published` but no route existed, so every one was a 404 — for Bella too). It reads `website_pages` through `getPageContent` (snapshot `readPageBySlug`, DB fallback `getPageBySlug({publishedOnly})`) and renders `SectionRenderer`. Static routes (menu, cart, reviews, ...) win over the dynamic segment; unknown/unpublished slug or `home` -> 404 (home is the restaurant root). A page is not linked anywhere automatically: add it to `websites.config.navigation.items` / footer columns (Zaytoun's seed links Our Story + Contact). A running server keeps the old snapshot until its refresh interval or a restart.
- **Functional pages are website pages too (2026-09-26).** `menu`, `reservation`, `reviews` and `locations` each have a `website_pages` row (same table/`sections` JSONB as home/about/contact; no schema change) so their layout is configurable. The new section type `page_content` (`{type, enabled, title?, subtitle?}`, `shared/contract/sections.ts`) marks where the built-in body (menu list + filters, booking form, reviews list + form, location cards) sits among the other sections, and `title`/`subtitle` override the body's own heading (defaults stay dynamic, e.g. the menu's "35 dishes available today"). Wiring: each route's `page.tsx` renders `<ConfiguredPage pageSlug=... render={(context, heading) => body}/>` (`components/storefront/configured-page.tsx`), which loads the row with `getPageContent`, and `SectionRenderer` takes a `body` prop; `generateMetadata` uses `configuredPageMetadata` (row `seo.title`/`title`/`description`, else the old defaults). **Fallback:** no row, unpublished, empty `sections`, or no/disabled `page_content` marker -> the body renders alone / after the other sections, so an existing restaurant without these rows behaves exactly as before (Bella's live DB got its four rows on 2026-09-26 by a targeted `insert ... on conflict (website_id, slug) do nothing`, not a reseed, which would have replaced Bella's data; the section arrays are `MENU_SECTIONS` etc. in `scripts/db/seed-data.ts`, shared by that insert and `db:seed`). Add a new functional page: add its slug to `FunctionalPageSlug`, split its `page.tsx` into `ConfiguredPage` + a `render...(context, heading)` function, seed the row with a `page_content` section. **Top photo vs. closing banner photo (2026-10-09):** both used to be `restaurant.coverUrl`, so the menu hero and the bottom "Feeding a crowd?" `cta` band showed the same picture. `page_content` now takes an optional `image` (editor field "Top background photo"); `ConfiguredPage` resolves it into `PageHeading.image` and the menu, reviews, locations and reservation heroes use `heading.image ?? coverUrl`. A `cta` band's own `image` now also wins over the cover when its style is `neutral` and it lands on night (before, only style `image` used it). Guarded by `tests/page-content-section.test.ts`. Seeds: `scripts/db/seed.ts` (Bella) and `scripts/db/zaytoun/seed/data.ts` (`MENU_SECTIONS` etc.).
- **Navigation items have an `enabled` flag (2026-09-26).** `websites.config.navigation.items[]` is `{label, href, enabled}` (`websiteConfigSchema`, `shared/contract/settings.ts`); `components/storefront/site-header.tsx` renders only `enabled` items (desktop and mobile share the list). A stored item without the key counts as enabled, so old configs keep every link and nothing needs migrating; to hide a link set `"enabled": false` in the DB, e.g. `update websites set config = jsonb_set(config, '{navigation,items}', (select jsonb_agg(case when i->>'label' = 'Reviews' then i || '{"enabled": false}' else i end) from jsonb_array_elements(config->'navigation'->'items') i)) where restaurant_id = (select id from restaurants where slug = 'zaytoun')` (the snapshot picks it up on the next refresh or restart). Seeds write `enabled: true` on every item (`BELLA.nav` in `scripts/db/seed-data.ts`, `NAV` in `scripts/db/zaytoun/seed/data.ts`); Bella's live row got `enabled: true` on all five items on 2026-09-26 via a targeted `jsonb_set` that only adds the key where it is missing. Footer columns are not flagged. Test: `tests/page-content-section.test.ts`.

## 19. UI design system (storefront + admin)

Presentation only; every colour, font and radius still comes from the restaurant's `websites.theme` (DB). Upgraded 2026-09-26 with the installed taste skills (`design-taste-frontend`, `redesign-existing-projects`, `high-end-visual-design`) as the checklist.

- **Theme root:** the storefront layout and the admin shell wrappers carry the class `theme-root` plus the inline `themeCssVariables(theme)`. Anything derived from brand variables must be declared on that element, never on `:root`: a `var()` inside a custom property resolves where it is declared (see section 18). `.theme-root` in `globals.css` defines `--radius-card` (1.5x brand radius, capped at 24px), `--radius-panel` (2x, capped at 32px), ink-tinted `--shadow-card`/`--shadow-raised`, `--shadow-brand` and the `font-family`.
- **Fonts bug fixed:** headings rendered in Georgia and body in system-ui for every restaurant, because `--font-display`/`--font-sans` resolved at `:root`. `themeCssVariables` now also emits them, and `FONT_STACKS` puts the next/font variables (`--font-display-fallback`, `--font-sans-fallback`) first for Playfair Display and Inter. Admin overrides `--font-display` with the body sans (no serif on a working tool).
- **Radius rule:** controls (buttons, inputs, chips inside forms) use `--radius-brand`; cards use `--radius-card`; large panels (CTA bands, hero-adjacent panels, auth shell) use `--radius-panel`.
- **Utilities (`globals.css`):** `surface-card` (border + surface + card radius + shadow), `surface-flat` (same, no shadow), `surface-tint`, `hover-lift`, `tabular` (tabular figures for all money), `eyebrow`, `scrollbar-none`, `reveal` (scroll-driven fade-up via `animation-timeline: view()`, gated by `@supports` and reduced motion, so other browsers just show content), `stagger-in` (set `--i`), `animate-sheet`, `animate-hero`. Element defaults (`h1-h4`, `p`, `:focus-visible`) live in `@layer base` so utilities can override them; an unlayered element rule beats every Tailwind utility.
- **Button variants:** `primary | secondary | outline | ghost | subtle | danger | link | inverse | glass`. `glass` is for buttons on photography (hero secondary CTA); `inverse` for buttons on a brand-coloured panel. `CtaSection` maps configured CTA styles to these automatically when the panel is coloured, because a primary button on a primary panel is invisible.
- **`--header-h`:** `site-header.tsx` publishes the sticky header's real height (announcement bar and paused notice included, open mobile sheet excluded) on `<html>`. The menu's sticky category rail, the item/cart sticky columns and category anchor offsets use `top-[var(--header-h,4.5rem)]`.
- **Section layouts:** hero (content anchored low, directional scrim, ken-burns), order-type switch (panel overlapping the hero edge only when it directly follows a hero: `SectionRenderer` sets `data-after=<previous section type>`), featured items (swipe rail on phones, grid above `sm`), menu categories and gallery (bento with a 2x2 feature tile; the last tile spans the remaining cells per breakpoint so rows never leave holes; no-image categories get a light brand-tint tile), why-choose-us (editorial split, no boxed cards), reviews (masonry via CSS columns, opened by the real rating summary). Spice level is `components/storefront/spice-level.tsx` (flame icons, no emoji). Auth pages use `components/storefront/auth-shell.tsx` (restaurant cover photo + form).
- **Grid overflow gotcha:** a `grid` with only `lg:grid-cols-[...]` has an auto track below `lg`, which grows to the widest nowrap child and pushes the page sideways on phones (seen on the item and cart pages). Always give a base `grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)]` and wrap `fr` tracks in `minmax(0,…)`.
- **Mobile audit pass 1 (2026-10-18):** a Playwright script (signed staff / super-admin / customer cookies, local DB, `next dev -p 3200` with `NEXT_DIST_DIR=.next-verify`) measured 42 routes at 320/375 for page overflow, tap targets < 36px, text < 11.5px and controls < 16px. Storefront had no overflow. Fixed: (1) **an `sr-only` inside an `overflow-x-auto` table scroller pushed the whole page sideways** (admin Payments +314px): `sr-only` is `position:absolute`, so it escapes a scroller that isn't its containing block; every admin table scroller is now `relative overflow-x-auto` — do the same for new ones. (2) The grid gotcha above in 13 places (super-admin Website settings, page editor, admin menu/settings/reports/order detail, menu sidebar, hero variants): all now have a base `grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)]`. (3) Variant/add-on rows in the item editor had fixed `w-48`/`w-40` inputs; the name now flexes (`min-w-0 flex-1`, fixed from `sm`). (4) **iOS zoom:** `.sa-root` forced every control to 14px and admin calls pass `text-sm`/`text-[13px]`, so Safari zoomed the page on every tap into a field; an unlayered `@media (max-width:639px)` rule in `globals.css` makes text controls ≥ 16px on phones (desktop sizes unchanged). (5) Admin category/item row icon buttons are `size-10` below `lg` (always visible there, no hover). (6) Editorial menu rows (`DishTile layout="row"`): on phones the price sits under the name; beside the 96px photo the shared row wrapped names to three lines. Full-page screenshots show a sticky bottom bar's natural slot as an empty band (dish page), which is not a real gap; check with a viewport screenshot.
- **Template pass (2026-10-18):** every variant (5 order-type doors, 5 heroes, 4 headers + their menu sheet, 5 menu layouts) switched in the local DB and shot at 320/375/430 (+768/1280 where layout changes); restart `next dev` with `STOREFRONT_CACHE_ENABLED=false` so config edits show without waiting for the snapshot. No overflow anywhere. Fixed: **grid menu `GridAddBar` clipped "Add to Cart" in every 2-column card** (nowrap `flex-1` button without `min-w-0`, hidden by the card's `overflow-hidden`, so overflow checks passed): it is a `@container` now, stepper over a full-width button below 14.5rem, one row above; the branch-gate hint button is `min-h-10` (long hints wrapped and clipped in `h-10`). **Framed hero** photo is `min(34svh,300px)` on phones (54svh filled the whole first screen; headline and buttons were below the fold), tighter photo→copy gap. **Capsule doors:** unselected discs are an ink tint, not solid ink (they outweighed the selected door); accent on hover/selected as before. **Cards doors:** slightly tighter on phones. Lesson: an element clipped by an `overflow-hidden` ancestor never shows as page overflow; look at the screenshots.
- **Typography system (2026-10-18; fix type here, not with per-component pixel values):**
  - **Display scale (phone-tuned, desktop unchanged):** `display-hero` `clamp(2.125rem,1.25rem+4.2vw,5.25rem)` (34px at 320, 36px at 375, three balanced lines instead of four); `display-1` page titles `clamp(1.75rem,1.2rem+2.3vw,3.25rem)` (28px phones); `display-2` section headings `clamp(1.4rem,1.2rem+1.1vw,2.2rem)` (~23px phones); `display-3` card/door titles unchanged (19px).
  - **`eyebrow`:** 12px, tracking 0.14em with a 1rem lead rule on phones (0.18em + 1.5rem from `md`); it was 11.2px / 0.22em and broke "Kitchen · City" after the dot at 320px.
  - **`label-caps`** (new): THE small uppercase label (form-group labels, "Today", dish meta, sidebar groups, photo badges): 12px, 0.08em, sans; sets size/case/tracking only, callers add weight and colour. Replaced 27 ad-hoc `text-[10.5–11.5px] uppercase tracking-[0.1–0.22em]` combos.
  - **Sizes:** the steps are 12 / 13 / 14 / 15 / 16 / 17px. Nothing readable is below 12px; 11px is only for count numerals in fixed discs (cart badge, sidebar counts). Half-pixel values (12.5/13.5/14.5/15.5px) were normalised up. The super-admin website preview thumbnail keeps 9–11px on purpose (a scale model). Branch pill name 10.5→12px and city 9→11px; branch strip 13px; dock labels, ETA chip, order-stage labels, `Badge` 12px; band/split door blurbs 14px.
  - **Admin / super admin titles:** 22px semibold (`text-[1.375rem]`, super admin `sm:text-2xl`), wrapping with `text-balance` + `overflow-wrap:anywhere` instead of truncating. The admin top-bar breadcrumb is hidden below `sm` (it truncated to "O…" beside the branch picker; the page title names the screen). The admin dashboard file is frozen and was not touched.
- **`whitespace-nowrap` did nothing for `<p>` children (root cause, fixed 2026-10-19):** `globals.css` base rules used the SHORTHAND `text-wrap: pretty` (p) / `text-wrap: balance` (h1–h4, display classes). The shorthand also sets `text-wrap-mode: wrap`, and since `<p>` declares it itself it beat the inherited `nowrap` from any `whitespace-nowrap` parent: admin order dates, "Cash on delivery" etc. wrapped to 3–4 lines inside nowrap table cells, everywhere in the app. Now the longhand `text-wrap-style`. Never use the `text-wrap` shorthand in a base/element rule (Tailwind's `text-balance`/`text-pretty` utilities on the element itself are fine).
- **Admin Orders list (2026-10-19):** cards up to `xl` (two-column grid from `md`), table from `xl`; payment method + state sit under the Total (no Payment column), Items capped at 13rem, so the table fits 1280 beside the sidebar with no inner scroll (measured: 942/942px). At `lg` the old table needed 1131px in a 762px column. Admin status tabs are 40px tall below `sm`; `AdminSwitch` / super-admin `Switch` keep their 44×24 look but have a ~52×44 hit area (`before:` pseudo).
- **Admin premium pass (2026-10-19, owner: "admin pages are simple, make every page premium and elegant, mobile and desktop").** Shared layer first, then each page. Dashboard untouched (frozen rule above, and it already matched).
  - **Canvas + elevation:** the content column is `--color-canvas-muted` (paper); `main.admin-main` scopes an UNLAYERED rule in `globals.css` that gives every `surface-card`/`surface-flat` inside it a soft two-layer shadow and a lighter hairline (`--admin-line`, `--admin-shadow`, `--admin-shadow-hover`); nested cards drop the shadow. Because the rule is unlayered, a utility can only opt out with `!` (e.g. `max-xl:shadow-none!`, `border-0!`).
  - **`AdminPageHeader`:** eyebrow = the nav group ("Service / Catalogue / Business", from `AdminBreadcrumb group`, so no page passes it), title in the restaurant's `--font-heading` serif 28/32px, a pill "Back" link on detail pages. All 38 headers get it for free. Descriptions pluralise ("1 zone", not "1 zones").
  - **New primitives (`admin-ui.tsx`):** `StatTile` (label + tinted icon disc that cannot collide, figure in the body SANS — serif old-style digits look uneven — `size="lg"` for a bento headline), `Avatar` (initials), `addTileClass` (dashed "+ Add …" tile that ends a card grid). `tableHead` = small-caps labels; `tableRow` hover = brand tint.
  - **Pages:** Orders = floating cards below `xl`; Menu = phone category chip row + "Manage categories" fold, 56px rounded dish photos; Reservations = time tile + name/status + one wrapping detail line (nothing truncates); Customers = phone card list, avatar table from `md`, sort as segmented control; Coupons = ticket cards (discount stub, code, usage meter) in a grid; Staff / Locations / Delivery zones = card grids (avatar or icon tile, zone facts Coverage·Fee·ETA) with add tiles, edit forms open full width (`col-span-full`); Reports = bento stats (Total sales spans two rows), joined Export pill group, readable dates, from–to pill; Payments = cards below `xl`, compact two-line table from `xl` (was 7 wrapping columns); Order detail = history timeline, cards top-aligned; Kitchen = lanes swipe (19rem snap columns) below `xl`, tinted lane wells, ticket ids never wrap; Customer detail = `StatTile`s + responsive order list; Item editor = sectioned (Details / Pricing & kitchen / Dietary) + aside (live photo preview, Visibility switches, save sticky on phones), lower sections in `AdminSection`s; Reviews = avatar + quote; Settings = `settings-nav.tsx` (icons + IntersectionObserver scroll-spy; phone pill row keeps the active pill in view).
  - Verified in Chrome via the iframe harness (`public/__harness.html`, temporary, deleted after) at 390 + 1100/1280 per page, plus an overflow sweep of all 17 admin routes at 360/390/768/1024/1280.
- **Admin inline editors (2026-10-19, owner: "delivery zone add/edit still basic"):** `components/admin/admin-form-panel.tsx` = `AdminFormPanel` (brand-tinted header with icon, title "Edit <name>" / "New …", close X; footer pinned with `sticky bottom-0` while the form is taller than the screen: `FormToggle` switches left, Cancel + "Save …" right) + `AdminFormSection` (title/description column beside the fields from `xl`, stacked below). Used by the zone, coupon, staff and location editors — same state, payloads and actions as before. Zone coverage is three option tiles (Area names / Radius / Draw on map), radius has a km suffix, ETA is "30 to 45 min"; coupon order types are toggle pills, % suffix on percent off; location "Primary branch" is a footer switch. `delivery-pricing-editor.tsx` is a ladder (header Range · Up to · Fee once; range chip "2–4 km"; on phones the chip sits above km + fee inputs — before, the range note collapsed into a one-word column at 390px). The dashed "+ Add" tile hides while another editor is open.
- **Figures are never cut with "…" (2026-10-19, owner: "PKR 112,47…" on Customer detail, phone and desktop):** `StatTile` had `truncate` on its value. Now the tile is a `@container` and the figure's font size is `clamp(min, 100cqi × 1.55 / units, max)` from `figureSize()` (units = digits + 2.2 for a money value, else the text length), so "38" stays 28px and a long amount shrinks only as far as needed; the currency code (`MONEY` = 2–4 capitals, `Rs`, or a symbol — words like "Oct 18" do not match) is set at 0.55em; a figure that still cannot fit wraps between code and number, `overflow-wrap:anywhere` only as a last resort; `title` carries the full value. Checked at 320/390/1100 incl. PKR 12,345,678.90 in a 320px phone tile. Same `truncate` removed from the delivery-zone Fee fact and the reservation confirmation facts. Rule: never `truncate` an amount, a date or a count. Also: breadcrumb / phone header no longer show a raw uuid for detail pages (`subPageLabel` → "Details"); Sales reports' custom range on phones (below `sm`) is `components/admin/report-range-picker.tsx`: one "Custom range / Select date range" button (shows the applied range) opening the shared bottom `Sheet` with a month calendar — first tap From, second tap To (a tap before From restarts), shaded band between, days after the restaurant's today disabled, Clear + "Show <range>" → `/reports?preset=custom&from=…&to=…` (To defaults to From). Date keys are handled in UTC so the viewer's zone cannot shift a day; `today` comes from `resolveReportRange("today", …, restaurant.timezone)`. `sm`+ keeps the compact native from–to pill. Checked 320/639/640/1100 and the full tap → apply → report flow.
- **Admin colour pass (2026-10-19, owner: "premium but too much white"):** colour now carries meaning, all from theme tokens. `.admin-column` (the content column, `globals.css`) paints a brand wash fading into the paper behind every page header (brand 17% → canvas-muted over 24rem) plus an accent glow in the top-right corner. `StatTile` takes its `tone` as `--tile`: solid tone icon disc, 3px tone top rule, soft tone corner glow. `statusEdge` + `statusEdgeStyle(tone)` (`admin-ui.tsx`, `TONE_VAR`) = a 4px left edge in the status colour via a `before:` pseudo (card borders are repainted by the unlayered `.admin-main` rule, so a border colour would lose) — on order cards and table rows, payment cards and rows, reservation rows, a customer's order history. `AdminSection` icons are brand-tinted; `AdminStatusTabs` active segment = brand text + accent underline, counts are pills; `Avatar` picks one of six theme hues from a hash of the name. Search / Apply buttons are the brand primary (were black / outline). Sales trend y-axis uses compact numbers ("100K"; full figures were cut to "00000" in the 44px axis).
- **Grid menu "Add" control (2026-10-19, owner: the full-width "+ Add" pill looked heavy; a first version put a round "+" beside the price and was rejected — an in-cart stepper is wider than the price column, so on phones it dropped to its own row and the two cards' prices sat at different heights):** `GridAddBar` (`grid-add-bar.tsx`, shown only by the grid layout's `DishTile inlineAdd`) is rendered by `dish-tile.tsx` on the PHOTO's bottom-right corner (`absolute bottom-2.5 right-2.5`), like Foodpanda / Uber Eats; the card footer is only the price (+ "From" for dishes with options, + the struck-through old price), so every price is on one line and lines up across a row (measured: footer height identical on all cards at 360/390/430). Not in the cart: round solid brand "+" (40px, 44px from `sm`, `before:` pseudo keeps the tap area larger; strong dark shadow so it reads on any photo). In the cart: `[🗑|− n +]` pill. Dishes with options: round OUTLINED "+" on a surface ground (hollow opens the dish; the "From" label says why). Branch / location gate: the worded pill ("Choose branch", "Set location", "Not available here"), as wide as its words, `max-w-full` of the photo. `aria-label`s unchanged ("Add <dish> to cart"). Other menu layouts are untouched.
- **Menu layout `cards` — "Product cards" (2026-10-19, owner's reference: a phone menu app with photo-left cards):** `page_content.menuLayout = "cards"` (super admin → Website → Menu page → content section → Menu layout → "Product cards — photo beside name, price tag and Add to Cart (best on phones)"; enum in `sections.ts`, `configured-page.tsx` type, `section-editor.tsx`, `tests/menu-layout.test.ts`). `menu/page.tsx` branch renders each category as a bold heading + a list of `components/storefront/product-card.tsx`: a raised card on the light grey `tone-muted` page, square photo on the left (100/112/128px), name, 2-line description, an accent-tinted price tag ("From" for dishes with options, struck-through old price, tiny diet / spice / prep-time line), then the add control. One column on phones, two from `md`, three from `xl`. The control is `GridAddBar wide` (`grid-add-bar.tsx`): solid worded "Add to Cart" button, the same `[🗑|− n +]` pill once in the cart, outlined "Customize" for dishes with options, the branch/location gate pill — the rules are the same code as the grid layout. Sold out / not at this branch: a grey label instead of the button. Theme colours only (button = brand, price tag = accent tint). The reference's heart icon is NOT built: the product has no favourites feature, so a heart would do nothing (add it together with a saved-dishes feature if wanted). Previewed by temporarily forcing the layout (the hosted DB was not touched), at 360/390/430/1100.
- **Admin phone header (2026-10-19, owner: "not premium on mobile"):** below `sm` the bar is 64px (56px from `sm`, still equal to the sidebar brand block at `lg`) and reads: round 40px ☰ (tint fill, no border) · restaurant initial disc (from 400px) + restaurant name in `--font-heading` with the CURRENT SCREEN under it (`AdminBreadcrumb compact`; phones had no "where am I" at all) · branch chip (40px pill, accent store icon from 360px, name capped 5.25rem/9rem and truncated) · 40px round sound toggle. `sm`+ keeps the bordered controls and the full breadcrumb. `AdminHeader` now takes `restaurantName`. Measured at 320/360/390/430: no overlap, header overflow 0.
- **Admin shell:** `admin-sidebar.tsx` exports `AdminSidebar` (desktop, lg+, grouped Service / Catalogue / Business, same permission gating) and `AdminMobileNav` (drawer). The drawer is portalled into `.theme-root` because the sticky header's `backdrop-filter` makes it the containing block for `position: fixed` children; any future overlay rendered inside a blurred header needs the same.
- **Spacing pass 2 (2026-10-11, requester: "still excessive spacing"):** measured, not guessed — a Playwright script loads each storefront page at 375/1280 against the running dev server and lists empty vertical bands ≥ 56px (scroll the page first or lazy `next/image` plates screenshot as empty grey boxes, which looks like dead space but is not). Root causes found: (1) the shared `section-y`/`section-body` were still generous, (2) every working page picked its own body padding (`pt-6..12`, `pb-12..20`), (3) `EmptyState` (py-10) stacked on that page padding, (4) the reservation form kept `pb-24` on phones for its fixed bar although content follows it (the footer already reserves dock space, `pb-[calc(1.5rem+4.5rem+…)]`). Now: `section-y` 2.5 / 3.25 (md) / 4rem (xl); `section-body` 1.5 / 2rem; new **`page-y`** (1.75rem top / 2.5rem bottom, md 2.25 / 3.5rem) for every working-page body under its `PageHero` and its `loading.tsx` (cart, checkout, orders, current orders, order tracking, dish page) — use it for new ones; `EmptyState` py-6; photo CTA bands `py-14 md:py-16`, reservation CTA `py-12 md:py-16`. Result: the largest storefront gaps went 224→120 px (phone reservation), 176→152 (desktop menu chapters), 189→165 (desktop home), every page shorter. **Home hero on phones (same day, follow-up):** `hero-section.tsx` `HEIGHTS` are mobile-first now — below `md` the hero is `lg` = `min(70svh,600px)` (`full` = `min(84svh,700px)`, `md` 62svh, `sm` 48svh) with the copy anchored low (`justify-end`, top padding header + 2.75rem, `pb-9` before the status bar, CTA `mt-7`, status bar `pt-5 pb-7`, and the `highlights` list is `hidden md:flex` — on phones it wrapped into a second and third line of small bullets under the status and read cluttered, so phones show the one "Open now · until · location" line); `md+` keeps the full-viewport heights. A first try at 68svh with tight padding read as congested (requester), and an 82svh try (after hiding highlights) left ~200px of blurred photo above the eyebrow that read as empty; 70svh keeps the photo band at ~100px (390×844), 52px (375×667), 133px (430×932). Measured hero: 390×844 844 → 591px (Pickup and Delivery visible in the first screen), no horizontal overflow. The rail's phone arrow row is `mt-3` (was `mt-6`). Admin (`main` px-4..10 py-6/8, 1.375rem titles, `space-y-6`) and super-admin were already dense and were not changed; the admin dashboard is frozen anyway. Admin could not be measured in a browser: the seeded `owner@bluehour.coffee` password did not sign in against the hosted DB.
- **Spacing scale (2026-09-26 refinement pass; fix spacing here, not per page):** `globals.css` owns the rhythm. `section-y` = a section's own padding, 3rem / 4rem (md) / 4.75rem (xl) (was 4.5/6.5rem; two stacked sections doubled it into ~200px of air; reduced again 2026-10-11, see above). `section-body` = heading group -> content, 1.75rem / 2.25rem (md); use it instead of `mt-10/12/14` after a `SectionHeading`. Inside a heading group: eyebrow `mb-3`, title -> lede `mt-3` (`SectionHeading` does this). Display scale is one step tighter (`display-hero` max 5.25rem, lh 1; `display-1` max 3.25rem; `display-2` 2.2rem; `display-3` 1.45rem; lede lh 1.6); functional page titles (cart, checkout, location names) use `display-1`, `display-hero` is for photo heroes and the live order status. Editorial splits use `gap-8 lg:gap-12/14` (not `gap-12 lg:gap-20`). `PageHero` is min(52svh,480px) / min(34svh,320px); the auth shell fills the viewport only on desktop (capped 46rem), never on phones. Admin screens were already dense and were left as they are.
- **Page rhythm (2026-09-26, `components/storefront/section-rhythm.ts`):** `SectionRenderer` computes one tone per section with `sectionTones()` and passes it as a `tone` prop. Some types own their ground (hero/about/reviews/reservation_cta/order_type_switch = night, featured/menu_preview/locations/page_content = paper, menu_categories/contact = muted, `cta` image = night, `cta` primary = brand); `why_choose_us`, `gallery`, `rich_text` and neutral `cta` are flexible and become night after a light section unless the next fixed section is already dark, else paper/muted alternating. So a configured page never runs as one long off-white stretch, with no DB change. A neutral `cta` that lands on night borrows `restaurant.coverUrl` as its photo. Tests: `tests/section-rhythm.test.ts`. New section types: add them to `FIXED` or `FLEXIBLE` and accept `tone?: SectionTone`.
- **Header actions (2026-09-26, at the requester's direction):** no "Order online" button (the menu is in the navigation). Right side, in order: **My orders** (signed-in customers only, to `/orders`), **Cart**, then the brand-filled account pill ("Sign In / Sign Up" to `/account/sign-in`, or the customer's initial + name to `/account`). My orders and Cart are `HeaderIconLink` (in `site-header.tsx`): an icon at rest that slides its label open on hover or keyboard focus (`group-hover/action`, `group-focus-visible/action`, max-width + opacity); the cart keeps `data-tray-target` (fly-to-tray target) and its count badge on the icon. On phones the bar keeps the cart icon and the menu button; the menu sheet has My orders (signed in) above the account button. `websites.config.ordering.ctaLabel` is no longer used by the header. To check a signed-in header without a sign-in write, mint a `rp_customer_session` cookie locally with `signCustomerSession` (`server/auth/tokens.ts`) for an existing customer row (read only).
- **Header tone is a DB setting (2026-09-26):** `websites.config.navigation.tone` = `"dark"` (the theme's night surface, i.e. `surfaceDark`) or `"light"` (the theme's background, frosted). Missing or invalid means `"dark"` (`z.enum(...).catch("dark")` in `websiteConfigSchema`), so existing restaurants are unchanged. Over a hero the header is always transparent with light type, and after scrolling it takes the configured ground; the mobile menu sheet follows the setting. No migration needed (JSONB). Switch a restaurant with `update websites set config = jsonb_set(config, '{navigation,tone}', '"light"') where restaurant_id = (select id from restaurants where slug = '<slug>')`, then restart (the snapshot cache parses the config at load). Seeds write `tone: "dark"` explicitly. Test: `tests/page-content-section.test.ts`.
- **Night colour per restaurant:** the header, dark bands and admin sidebar use `--color-night` = `theme.surfaceDark`, else `secondary` mixed with near-black. A cool `secondary` gives a cold slate that can clash with a warm palette, so set `surfaceDark` explicitly: Bella Napoli got `#1C1512` (warm espresso; cream text ~17:1, gold accent ~9:1) on 2026-09-26 via `theme || '{"surfaceDark": "#1C1512"}'` on its `websites` row (one-row update, migrator connection) and in `scripts/db/seed-data.ts`. Restart or wait for the snapshot refresh to see it.
- **Section layouts, round 2:** why-choose-us = sticky heading left + numbered ruled ledger right; gallery = magazine spreads of three (one large plate + two stacked, alternating side; 2 left over = side by side, 1 = panorama), so there are never masonry holes (`section.columns` is no longer used for layout); rich text = title column + serif lede with drop cap.
- **Functional pages all open on `PageHero`** (menu, reviews with the real average as the hero figure, locations, My Orders, Current Orders); reservation is a split: sticky night photo panel (cover image, notes, phone) + a three-step form (date chips strip, guest stepper, slots grouped Morning/Afternoon/Evening, sticky summary bar on phones; the mobile dock is hidden on `/reservation` like on cart/checkout). The e2e test selects a date with `getByRole("radiogroup", { name: "Date" }).getByRole("radio")`. Locations = one full-width band per kitchen alternating paper/night with a live `serviceStatus` line and the hours table. Order tracking = the live pass (status headline + stations rail, accent-coloured fill) on a night band, order details on paper. Cart/checkout summaries are a night "ticket"; empty cart page is a night panel over the cover photo.
- **Wording and heading refinement (2026-09-29, at the requester's direction):**
  - **"Cart", never "Tray", in anything a customer reads.** Headings say **My Cart**; buttons, toasts, empty states, the checkout breadcrumb, the sheet title, metadata and customer-facing server messages (`services/cart.ts`, `repositories/orders.ts`) say "cart". The storage is still the tray cookie, so code names (`shared/tray.ts`, `readTray`, `data-tray-target`, `fly-to-tray`, `rp_tray_*`) are unchanged: rename only text.
  - **Order labels say My Orders** (title case): header icon link, mobile menu sheet, profile drawer, the order page link, the `/orders` hero and metadata, and the mobile dock (dock labels `truncate`, so it fits at 320px).
  - **Quieter headings on working pages.** `PageHero size="sm"` (Orders, Current Orders, Reviews, Locations) renders its title at `clamp(1.9rem, …, 3rem)` in regular weight. `md` (menu) keeps the big one. The cart page title is `display-2 font-normal`, with `pt-8 md:pt-12` above the header block and the same rhythm on `/orders`. The home reviews section heading is `display-2`. Mobile menu-sheet links are 1.4rem.
  - **Reviews on phones:** the hero rating figure sits beside its stars (a row below `lg`, right-aligned stack at `lg`); the spotlight quote is `display-3 md:display-2`; list titles are sans semibold; every review text has `break-words`. Home review cards are `p-5` on phones, with the reply clamped to 3 lines, because rail cards share one height and a long reply stretched them all.
  - **Header action pills:** `HeaderIconLink` (**My Orders**, **My Cart**) is an icon at rest that **slides open** into a labelled pill on hover or focus, at every width. Keep that slide: the requester wants it, and a 2026-09-29 change that showed the labels permanently on `lg` was reverted. Opened, the pill is `pl-3 pr-4`, with `padding` in the transition. A link with a `badge` (the cart count) gets `ml-3` before its label instead of `ml-2`, because the badge overhangs the icon's right edge (`-right-1.5`) and otherwise touched the "M".
  - **Mobile dock = Home · Menu · Book · (Track | My Orders | Sign in). There is no cart button.** The header's cart icon (top right, with its count) is always visible on phones and is the fly-to-cart target (`flyToTray` picks among all `[data-tray-target]`, so removing the dock's copy is safe). Home is active only on `/` itself (`exact`); other tabs are active on their sub-pages too. `MobileDock` no longer takes `showCart`.
  - **Mobile menu sheet:** 17px serif links (`py-3`), 16px arrows at 60% opacity (full on hover or when active), a small dot before the current page, 44px (`h-11`) action buttons, and a 40px phone row.
  - Checked with Playwright at 320, 375 and 1280 px (no horizontal overflow) against a second `next dev` on port 3100 with `NEXT_DIST_DIR=.next-verify`, so a running `next start` on `.next` is untouched. Use `chromium.launch({ channel: "chrome" })` when Playwright's own browser is not downloaded.
  - A `next build` failing in `next/font` with "Cannot read properties of null (reading '1')" is a failed Google Fonts download; retry.
- **Shared empty state:** `components/storefront/empty-state.tsx` (`EmptyState`) for every empty / blocked state (cart, orders, current orders, reviews, unknown order). Use it instead of ad-hoc boxes.
- **Status colours are theme tokens only:** no Tailwind `red-/emerald-/amber-/green-/sky-` classes anywhere in `app/` or `components/`; use `var(--color-success|warning|danger|info)` (from the theme, with fallbacks in `web/theme.ts#semanticTokens`), mixed with `color-mix` for tints.
- **Checkout city** is prefilled from the primary location's city (`defaultCity` prop), no longer a hardcoded "Lahore".
- **Admin:** the top bar (`admin-header.tsx`) is `tone-night` too and both it and the sidebar brand block are `h-[4.75rem]`, so the bar and rail form one dark frame with their rules aligned (change both heights together). The sidebar and mobile drawer are `tone-night` (active item = tint + accent bar; brand name in the restaurant's serif `--font-heading`). The dashboard is a night "pass" panel (date, sentence, status tiles; pending is accent-filled) plus live panels gated by permission: latest orders (`orders.view`), upcoming reservations (`listReservationsForStaff` upcoming, `reservations.view`), reviews to moderate (`listReviewsForAdmin` pending, `reviews.view`). The admin menu table shows dish thumbnails (`resolveMenuImage`).
- **Postgres `date` columns (fixed 2026-09-26):** node-postgres parses a `date` column into a JS `Date` at **local** midnight, so the old `str(row.reservation_date).slice(0, 10)` produced "Sat Sep 26" (no year) instead of `2026-09-26`: the admin reservations list showed it raw, the dashboard showed "Invalid Date", the booking success screen and reservation emails fell back to the raw text, and `analytics.ts` sales series had the same bug on `::date`. Use `dateOnly()` (`server/db/mappers.ts`; reads local calendar fields, never `toISOString()`, which shifts the day east of UTC such as PKT) for every `date` column, or `to_char(..., 'YYYY-MM-DD')` in SQL (as `getBookedSlotCounts` does). Display a date key with `formatDateKey()` (`shared/hours.ts`, UTC-noon so the viewer's zone cannot move the day); reservation emails, the success screen, the admin list and dashboard all use it. Test: `tests/date-only.test.ts` (run it with `TZ=Asia/Karachi` too).
- **Verifying visually:** take full-page screenshots with `reducedMotion: "reduce"`, otherwise scroll-reveal sections below the fold are captured at opacity 0. Wait for hydration (a few seconds on `next dev`) before clicking client buttons, or the click does nothing. Long screenshot sessions can exhaust `next dev`'s heap; start it with `NODE_OPTIONS=--max-old-space-size=6144`. In Git Bash set `MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1` or `/menu`-style arguments are rewritten into Windows paths.
- **Checkout order summary lists the items (2026-10-03).** The night "Order summary" ticket used to go straight from its heading to the price breakdown, so the only sign of what was being ordered was "N items from X" in the page header. It now lists every tray line (`quantity×`, name, variant/add-ons joined with ` · `, line total) above the breakdown. `checkout/page.tsx` maps `view.lines` (the `TrayLineView`s it already priced) into `CheckoutForm`'s new `items: CheckoutSummaryItem[]` prop — display only, no new read; quantities are still changed on `/cart`.
- **Checkout redesign (2026-10-07, at the requester's direction), fully theme-driven.** A first version shipped a fixed ivory/green/brass palette (`checkout-theme.ts`, `--co-*` tokens). That broke this section's rule (every restaurant's checkout turned green), so it was removed the same day. **Never add a page-level palette: every colour, radius and shadow is a theme token.** The mapping:
  - **Fills:** `--color-brand`/`-foreground` for selected cards, the summary header band, the Place order button and the progress "current" dot. `--color-brand-accent`/`--color-brand-accent-foreground` for the selected check, the "done" step and the summary's accent strip.
  - **Tints:** `--brand-tint` for icon tiles and the Total due block, `--tint` for the address card and read-only email.
  - **Base:** `--color-surface`/`--color-ink`/`--color-muted-ink`/`--color-hairline`/`--rule-strong`.
  - **Radii and shadows (2026-10-08, aligned with the rest of the site at the requester's direction):** the section cards are flat (border only, like `surface-card`). Only the order summary is raised (`--shadow-card`, `--radius-panel`, like the cart's ticket). The selected order-type card has no glow. Place order is the site's primary button: `--radius-control` pill, no shadow at rest, `--shadow-brand` on hover. The payment note is a flat `--steel-2` tint like the cart's notices. The title is `display-2 font-normal` with a closing `--rule`, the same as My Cart. Controls inside the cards keep `--radius-brand`. Previewed by injecting the classes into the live page before implementing; computed styles were then verified on an isolated build: no shadow on all 6 cards, title 32.32px = My Cart.

  Small accent-coloured text uses brand or muted ink instead, because an accent can be icon-only contrast (Bun Intended's is 3.2:1). Header, footer, `globals.css` and the shared UI primitives were not touched. Layout: numbered cards (01 order type as large radio cards, stacked icon-over-text when three types share a row, 02 details, 03 delivery address / pickup branch / table, 04 payment), then tip and notes cards, with a sticky summary on the right (`lg`, 1.7fr/1fr): a brand header band, item thumbnails (`CheckoutSummaryItem.imageUrl` = `resolveMenuImage(line.imageUrl, null)`, placeholder icon otherwise), totals, a tinted "Total due" block and the Place order button.
  - **Other hardcoded colours removed in the same pass (audit of `src/`):**
    - admin `zone-coverage-map.tsx` (shape colour was `#1f6f50`; it now reads `--color-brand` from its container with `getComputedStyle`, because Maps needs a real colour string);
    - `spice-level.tsx` (flames were `#c2410c`; now `--color-warning`);
    - the menu hero's active order-type pill (was `bg-white text-[#111]`; now `--brand-surface`/`--brand-foreground`, the raw theme values that tone sections do not remap).
  - **Deliberately left:** schema defaults (`shared/contract/settings.ts`), the `:root` fallbacks in `globals.css` and `theme.ts`, `var(--token, #fallback)` defaults, black scrims and shadows over photos, the root `app/not-found.tsx` (no restaurant context) and the admin PDF/XLSX exports.
  - **Known gap: emails use `restaurants.primary_color`, not `websites.theme`.** Text on the header and button is always `#fff`. As of 2026-10-07 the two values match for every restaurant except Sakura (null, so emails fall back to `DEFAULT_COLOR`).
