No kitchen owned it, and every branch-scoped staff member saw it (`branchFilter` includes null rows). Now:
  - with `features.BranchingFeature` on and no `locationId`, the order is refused with `BRANCH_UNAVAILABLE` ("Please choose the branch for this order on the menu…") inside the transaction, before any write;
  - with the feature off and delivery, an order without a named branch records the serving zone's branch (`locationId ??= zoneRecord.locationId`) instead of null.

- **Single-branch mode = flag off (fixed 2026-10-12): every order is the PRIMARY branch's.**
  - **Bug:** with the flag off on a restaurant with several locations (Zaytoun), checkout showed the dead-end page "We cannot take this order yet / Select a delivery area to continue" with no way to pick an area.
  - **Root cause:** the tray had no `locationId`, so checkout loaded every branch's zones. `priceTray` only previews a zone when it belongs to the tray's branch or when all zones are one branch's, so pricing threw `DELIVERY_ZONE_REQUIRED` before the form (which holds the Delivery area select) could render.
  - **Fix: one rule, `shared/branching.ts#orderBranchId(enabled, trayLocationId, primaryId)`, applied where the tray is read (`web/storefront.ts#readTray`).** Flag on: the tray's chosen branch (unchanged). Flag off: `primaryLocation` (`is_primary`, else the first active), whatever the cookie says.
  - **What follows from it:** cart, checkout and `placeOrder` → `createOrder` all agree. Zones, hours, menu availability and the order's `location_id` are the primary branch's. A branch id left in the cookie while the flag was on (or edited by hand) is ignored, and the client re-adopts the pinned id.
  - **Checkout with the flag off:** passes only `[primaryLocation]` to `CheckoutForm`, so the old section-21 in-form branch picker / "Please choose a branch" never appears.
  - **No delivery zones at all for that branch:** the blocking page now says to switch to pickup.
  - **Unserviceable addresses:** `createOrder` refuses them with "Sorry, we don't deliver to that address yet. Check the area and city, or choose pickup." (flag on keeps "this branch does not deliver…").
  - **No locations:** `orderBranchId` returns null and the zone's branch is recorded, as before.
  - **Tests:** `tests/branching.test.ts` ("the branch an order belongs to") and `tests/create-order-sql.test.ts` (single-branch delivery + refusal wording, both flag states).
  - **Browser-verified:** a throwaway `next dev -p 3100 NEXT_DIST_DIR=.next-verify` with a temporary env-guarded `BranchingFeature` override in `assembleStorefrontContext`, removed afterwards (also remove the `.next-verify/types` line that `next dev` adds to `tsconfig.json`). Flag off: the old code dead-ends and the fix renders the form with the primary branch's areas; a stale `…0002` branch cookie is pinned back to `…0001`. Flag on: auto-pick and the locked branch are unchanged, and an out-of-city branch in the cookie still redirects to the menu.
  No extra query: `features` and the zones' `location_id` are already in the one read. Tests: `tests/create-order-sql.test.ts` ("the order always has the branch that cooks it"); they fail without the fix. Browser-verified against the hosted DB (refusals throw before the insert, so the transaction rolls back): a tampered cookie with no branch and an Islamabad branch for a Lahore address were both refused with a toast, and the customer's order count was unchanged.
- **Two cookies, neither trusted for money.** The DESTINATION is `rp_dest_<slug>` (`shared/branching.ts`: `DeliveryDestination` = label/line1/area/city/postalCode/lat/lng/addressId; tolerant `decodeDestination`; written by the action through `web/storefront.ts#writeDestination`, 60 days). The BRANCH is the tray cookie's `locationId`, as it always was (section 20), which is why cart, checkout and the order needed no new plumbing.
- **Serviceability = the order's own rule** (`server/services/branching.ts#rankBranches`, pure): a branch serves a destination when one of ITS active delivery zones matches the address via `matchDeliveryZone` (area / city / postal code) — exactly what `createOrder` checks for `input.locationId` (its zone read is already filtered by `$5`). So a branch offered on the menu can never be refused at checkout for coverage, and there is still no second zone system (no radius, no polygon). Distance is straight-line from the DESTINATION to the branch and only sorts; a typed address without coordinates still gets its serviceable branches, unsorted. The customer's own GPS position is never an input: Karachi GPS + a Lahore address returns Lahore branches. **To make a branch serve a whole city, list the city name as a zone area** (the haystack is `"area, city"`).
- **Order types:** delivery uses the zone-matched list (`BranchingState.delivery`); pickup / dine-in may choose any active branch (`.all`, nearest first once a location is known). `branchesFor(state, orderType)` picks. On the menu the tray's order type is the active tab unless `?orderType=` is explicit, in which case `BranchBar` writes it to the tray — so bar, add controls and cart talk about the same order.
- **State per request:** `web/storefront.ts#getBranching(context)` (React-`cache()`d; destination cookie + snapshot) -> the layout passes it to `components/storefront/branching.tsx#BranchingProvider` (inside `LocalCartProvider`). `useBranching()` gives `gate(orderType)` = `"ok" | "need-location" | "no-service" | "need-branch"`, `canOrder`, `isOffHere(itemId)`, `openLocation`, `openBranches`, `resolveGate`. The provider owns the two `Sheet`s: the location sheet (saved addresses loaded lazily with `getProfileAction` when it opens — the menu still makes no database read; `LocationPicker` when a Maps key exists; **typed Area/City always offered**, because a key without billing cannot geocode — section 21) and the branch sheet (radio list, "Nearest" badge, distances).
- **Location sheet city / area dropdowns (2026-10-12):** `BranchingState.deliveryAreas` (`services/branching.ts#rankBranches`) = per delivery city, the area names of its active branches' active zones (trimmed, de-duplicated case-insensitively, sorted, the city's own name dropped since that means "whole city"). `LocationChooser` (`branching.tsx`) shows City and Area as `Select`s from it, each with "Another city…" / "My area isn't listed…" revealing a text field (radius / drawn zones have no names; pickup can be anywhere); a remembered destination preselects the listed spelling. Submit needs a city, and an area when the city lists any. No area list (no named zones) = the old two text fields. `deliveryCities` now keeps the FIRST spelling when branches spell a city differently (it used to keep the last). Tests: `tests/branching.test.ts` ("deliveryAreas"). Browser-checked on Blue Hour (Lahore, 18 areas) at 390/1280 px.
- **Auto-pick rules (in the provider):** a new destination moves the tray to the nearest serviceable branch — unless the current branch also serves it and moving would drop cart items, then it stays. A destination + serviceable list with no valid branch in the tray (first visit, order type changed) takes the nearest silently, again never when that would drop items. Pickup with several branches and no location is a deliberate choice (no auto-pick).
- **One cart, one branch.** Items are shared across branches; a branch only switches items off (`menu_item_location_overrides`, section 22). The snapshot now carries `branchUnavailable` (location id -> item ids; `repositories/menu.ts#listBranchUnavailableItems`, one more query in `loadStorefrontData`; the admin toggle already invalidates the cache). Switching branch with cart lines that are off at the new branch shows the shared confirm dialog ("Switch to X? A, B are not available at this branch and will be removed") and removes exactly those lines; declining keeps cart and branch. `getOrderableMenuItems(restaurantId, ids, tray.locationId)` flags such a line in the cart preview too — this now applies with the feature off as well (once checkout has put a branch in the tray), matching what the order always enforced.
- **Gated surfaces:** `QuickAddButton` (pin icon -> opens the missing step; switched off with `aria-disabled` when no branch delivers), `ItemCustomizer` (button reads "Choose location" / "Choose branch" / "No delivery here" / "Not at this branch"), `DishTile` (`browseOnly` on the home sections `featured-items` / `menu-preview`: the tile links to `/menu#<categorySlug>`, and since 2026-10-08 the add control is `quick-add-button.tsx#BranchReadyQuickAdd`, the normal `QuickAddButton` shown only once `canOrder()` (a serving branch is chosen, e.g. from the header pill) and the dish is not `isOffHere`; before that, no add control. Client-side because the home sections are server components without the branch state; `offAtBranch`: greyed with "Not at this branch"), `LocalCartPanel` ("Ordering from <branch> · Change", a warning + no checkout while the gate is not ok), checkout page (redirects to `/menu` without a serviceable branch) and `CheckoutForm` (`branch` prop: branch shown fixed with a "Change" link, the old city-based branch cards and auto-select are skipped, and the destination preselects the saved address by `addressId` or prefills "new address").
- **Admin:** superseded by section 24 (the per-page `BranchFilter`/`?location=` is gone; the menu's branch view follows the header's branch scope whenever there are 2+ branches, flag or not).
- **`useConfirm` moved** from `components/admin/confirm-dialog.tsx` to `components/ui/confirm-dialog.tsx` (the storefront uses it now) and portals into `.theme-root` like `Sheet` — on `<body>` it sat outside the theme variables and fell back to default fonts.
- **Testing:** `tests/branching.test.ts` (DB-free: zone-based serviceability, distance order, destination-not-GPS, no-service, inactive branch/zone, postal code, typed address, pickup list, cookie codec, flag default and merge). Browser-verified on Zaytoun at 1280 and 375 px without writing to the database: the flag was defaulted to `true` in the schema for a throwaway `next dev` on port 3100 and reverted. **To switch it on for real:** admin → Settings → Features → "Multi-branch ordering", then restart (the snapshot parses features at load).
- **Not verified in a browser (needs database writes):** the branch-switch confirmation with real conflicting items and the admin toggle's save — Zaytoun has no override rows yet. Switch one item off at one branch in admin, add it to the cart at the other branch, then change branch.
- **Not built (ask before adding):** per-branch prices or per-branch categories (availability only); a one-tap "current location" on the menu bar itself (it lives inside the location sheet's map picker).

## 24. Admin branch access: one branch scope + write guard (2026-10-06 re-audit)

- **Who sees what:** `team_members.location_id` is the single source (same column RLS 0027 reads). null = restaurant-wide (owner/admin); a value = branch-scoped (manager/staff). `server/auth/branch-scope.ts` holds the rules (`resolveBranchScope`, `canAccessBranch`, `assertRestaurantWide`, `canManageMember`); `web/admin.ts#getAdminBranchScope(slug)` is what every branch-scoped page/route calls. Branch staff are always pinned to their branch; owner/admin pick one in the header (`BranchSwitcher`, cookie `rp_admin_branch` path `/admin`, value = location id or `all`), default = primary branch (`is_primary`, else first by `is_primary desc, sort_order, name`). One branch = no selector, unfiltered. **Never read a branch from the URL** (`tests/branch-access.test.ts` checks the admin tree for it).
- **Filtering is in SQL:** `db/mappers.ts#branchFilter(alias, "$n")` = that branch + rows with no branch (same rule as RLS `can_access_location`; it throws if the placeholder is not `$n`). Applied to orders (list, counts, recent, kitchen, activity poll, sales report, export), reservations, payments, reviews, customer detail stats/history, delivery zones, team list (strict: only that branch's members).
- **Restaurant-wide data** (menu catalogue: categories/items/variants/add-ons/item-level availability; coupons) is written by owner/admin only: actions use `requireRestaurantWidePermission`. Branch managers change only their branch's item availability (`menu-admin.ts#setItemLocationAvailability` checks branch + restaurant + item) and their branch's delivery zones (`delivery-zones.ts` checks the STORED zone's branch).
- **Menu list availability switch (2026-10-09).** (1) Slow click: `item-row-actions.tsx` waited for the action and then called `router.refresh()`, but the action already `revalidatePath`s `/menu`, so the force-dynamic page rendered twice per click against the hosted DB. The switch now uses `useOptimistic` inside an async transition (instant, falls back by itself on refusal, and follows the server's props afterwards: a plain `useState(initial)` kept stale values when the header scope or another admin changed them) with no refresh; `category-manager.tsx` had the same double render on save/delete and lost its refreshes too. Rule: an admin action that revalidates the current page needs no `router.refresh()`. (2) Owner/admin in the header's "All branches" scope with 2+ branches: the switch opens a popover "Where is X available?" with an "All branches" switch (`menu_items.is_available`, `toggleItemAvailabilityAction`) and one switch per branch (`setItemLocationAvailabilityAction`, disabled while the item is off everywhere). The page loads every branch's switched-off items with `menu-admin.ts#listBranchUnavailableItemsForAdmin` (only in that view), and the row reads "Off at <branch>" / "Off at N branches". With one branch, or a branch in scope, the switch acts directly as before. The admin menu page's reads (categories, items, branches, branch overrides, all-branch off list) run in ONE `Promise.all` decided from `getAdminBranchScope` (its `choices` are already the active branches; only locked staff re-read them); before, the branch list was re-read after categories/items and the overrides waited on it — three sequential DB rounds instead of one. **Still slow, second pass (2026-10-09):** the spinner waited for `revalidatePath("/menu")` re-rendering the whole page (all its reads) into each action's answer, while the write itself is one statement. `toggleItemAvailabilityAction` and `setItemLocationAvailabilityAction` no longer revalidate anything (in Next 15 ANY `revalidatePath` in an action, even of another path, re-renders the open page into the response). Every caller keeps its own state (`ItemRowActions`, `BranchItemToggle`, `BranchAvailabilityManager`), and admin pages are dynamic, so a later visit reads fresh. All three use `components/admin/use-synced-state.ts#useSyncedState(id, serverValue)` (not `useOptimistic`, which would snap back to the stale props once the transition ended): local state that adopts new server props whenever they change, and remembers the last value set per id in module scope so a back/forward visit (Next replays the router's cached, older props) still shows what was saved; fresh server props always win. Pass it a value whose JSON is stable (sorted arrays/keys). A refused save in `ItemRowActions` undoes only its own change, not later successful clicks. The branch popover closes as soon as any of its switches is picked.
- **Roles (migration 0029 + `permissions.ts`):** manager lost `settings.view` (Settings = owner/admin only), gained `staff.manage`. `assignableRoles`: owner→all, admin→admin/manager/staff, manager→staff only. `services/team.ts`: a manager's hire always goes to the manager's branch; owner/admin must pick a branch for manager/staff; owner/admin are saved with no branch; nobody edits their own role/branch (name/phone ok) or anyone they could not assign; adding an email already on the team is a CONFLICT (no silent upsert). **Fixed: `createTeamMember` used to overwrite the password of any existing login with that email (cross-restaurant account takeover); an existing login now keeps its password.**
- **Write guard (0029):** admin writes run as `app_service` (no RLS) and many update `where id = $1`, so 0027 never protected them (and not even the tenant). Trigger `app.guard_scoped_write` on orders/reservations/reviews/delivery_zones/menu_item_location_overrides/restaurant1s/team_members (+ tenant-only on payments/menu tables/coupons) raises SQLSTATE `RB403` (mapped to FORBIDDEN in `errors.ts`) for a row of another restaurant or, for branch staff, another branch. `updateOrderStatus`/`updateReservationStatus`/`moderateReview` also carry the predicate in SQL (out of scope = NOT_FOUND). 0029 also clears `location_id` on owner/admin rows; the four demo seeds now do the same.
- **Pages:** admin `page.tsx` files use `web/session.ts#requireAdminPage` — a refused page redirects to the dashboard with a "no access" notice (`?denied=1`; under a loading boundary the redirect is streamed, HTTP 200 + NEXT_REDIRECT). Actions/routes keep FORBIDDEN (export answers 403).
- **Verified 2026-10-06:** 0029 dry-run in a rolled-back transaction on the hosted DB (12 tamper scenarios as Villa manager/owner); every scoped read run read-only against the dev DB as Zaytoun manager/owner (45 calls); live `next start` probe as owner/manager/staff incl. forged cookie and `?location=`. **0027, 0029 and 0030 are applied on the hosted dev DB (checked 2026-10-08; stored checksums match the files).** Any other database needs `npm run db:migrate` before this code is deployed: zone saves write 0030's columns, and the DB-level guard and SQL-side role change only exist after 0029.
- **Data to fix:** Zaytoun has an active `staff` member with no branch (acts restaurant-wide under RLS); the Staff page flags such rows "No branch".
- **Checkout "Save this address for next time" (2026-10-06).** Signed-in customer + delivery + "Use a new address": a checkbox (unticked by default since 2026-10-07, at the requester's direction: saving is opt-in; the name field only appears once it is ticked) and a name field ("Home" for the first address, else "Other") in `checkout-form.tsx`; sent as `placeOrderSchema.saveAddressAs` (empty = don't save). `checkout/actions.ts#placeOrderAction` calls `services/customer-profile.ts#rememberCheckoutAddress` inside `after()` — outside the ONE order transaction (section 20), so it adds no work before the order and a failed save never fails it (logged, never thrown). It validates with `customerAddressSchema` (incomplete address = silently not saved), skips a duplicate (same street/area/city, case-insensitive) and makes the customer's first address the default. Picked map coordinates are saved too. Test: `tests/checkout-save-address.test.ts`.
- **The branch lives in the global header, not on the menu (2026-10-07, at the requester's direction; replaces the earlier menu "Delivering to / Serving from / Change location" strip).** `components/storefront/header-branch-pill.tsx#HeaderBranchPill` is rendered by `site-header.tsx` on every storefront page.
  - **Placement (2026-10-11, at the requester's direction):** desktop (`lg+`) = rightmost action, after the account button (`hidden lg:flex`). Below `lg` it is **`variant="strip"`**: a full-width 40px bar inside `<header>` under the logo row — "📍 Serving from / Pick up at / Dining at **<branch>** · <city> … Change ⌄" (city from 360px; unserved = warning tint, "Not available in <destination>"), same popover. It **hides while scrolling down and returns on any scroll up** (`site-header.tsx` `stripHidden`: rAF-throttled, 8px dead zone, always shown above 96px and on navigation). The collapse is `grid-rows-[1fr]→[0fr]` + opacity on a wrapper (`data-branch-strip`, `inert` while hidden), so the header's ResizeObserver republishes `--header-h` and sticky rails (menu category rail) slide up with it; the popover closes on window scroll. Verified in a browser with a minted `rp_dest_blue-hour` cookie (`encodeDestination`; the branch auto-picks): header 110px at top → 69px scrolled down → 110px on scroll up, popover opens from the strip, nothing at `lg`. Two mounts in `site-header.tsx`, only one ever displayed. Once the pill shows (`locationSet` = branching on and gate not `need-location`/`need-branch`), the account button collapses to a `HeaderIconLink`-style icon; before that it stays the brand-filled "Sign In / Sign Up" pill.
  - **Look (2026-10-11, matched to the requester's reference):** rounded-full pill, faint accent tint fill + ~30% accent border; outline pin (accent, stroke 1.75) → hairline accent divider → two lines (branch name, then city in accent-tinted muted ink) → accent chevron. `h-10` on `lg` = the Sign In / Sign Up button; `h-9` below. City shows from 400px wide (always on `lg`); long names truncate. All colours are theme tokens.
  - **State:** it reads only `useBranching()` (`selected`, `branches`, `gate()`, `destination`, `deliveryCities`) and `useLocalCart().cart.orderType`. It holds no branch state of its own.
  - **Four states:**
    - branch chosen → "📍 <branch> · <city> ▾" opens a Radix popover: "Serving from" (Pick up from / Dining at), "Delivering to <destination>", the eligible `branches` as radios (current one checked, "Nearest", distance), and "Change delivery location →";
    - `need-location` → "Select location" opens the location sheet;
    - `no-service` → the destination with a warning outline; the popover gives the old strip's "Ordering isn't available in X yet. We currently deliver in …";
    - `need-branch` → "Choose branch" opens the branch sheet.
  - **Size (2026-10-08, the requester picked option B from a three-way preview):** the trigger is 32px tall (`h-8`), 12px text, a 14px pin and a 12px chevron, `pl-2 pr-2.5`, `gap-1.5`, for every state (branch, Select location, Choose branch, unserved). The popover is unchanged. Measured: 142×32 at 1600px, 90×32 at 1280px and 390px, 32×32 at 1024px; clickable everywhere, no overflow.
  - **Width priority:**
    - `xl+`: pin + name + chevron;
    - `2xl` (and `md` on tablets): adds "· city";
    - 1024–1279 (`lg:max-xl`): a 32px pin square with no divider. Measured: with Blue Hour's 6 nav items only ~30px is left beside the logo at 1024.
    - The full "Branch · City" is always in `title` and `aria-label`, and the logo is `shrink-0`.
  - **Switching:** the popover calls the provider's new `pickBranch(branch)`. The branch sheet uses it too; it wraps the existing `switchBranch` (confirm before dropping cart items, tray cookie, `router.refresh()`) plus the "Ordering from X" toast, and refuses any branch not in the current `branches` list.
  - **Theme and motion:** the popover is portalled into `.theme-root` (like `Sheet`/`useConfirm`), uses only theme tokens, and enters with `animate-popover` (new `globals.css` keyframe, 160 ms, scales from `--radix-popover-content-transform-origin`; covered by the global reduced-motion block).
  - **Menu:** `branch-bar.tsx` now only exports `MenuOrderTypeSync`. It renders nothing and keeps the strip's one non-visual job: an explicit `?orderType=` tab becomes the tray's order type. The page flow is header → hero/search → sticky category rail → sections.
- **Bug fixed 2026-10-07 (`local-cart.tsx`): the first-visit nearest-branch auto-pick never stuck.** React runs a child's effects before its parent's on mount. `BranchingProvider` (child) committed the nearest branch, then `LocalCartProvider` (parent) undid it:
  - its "adopt the server's reading" effect re-applied the mount-time `initial`;
  - its "persist" safety net wrote the render-time `cart` (the old tray) back to the cookie.

  So a tray holding a branch that does not serve the destination (e.g. an Islamabad branch for a Lahore address) kept it. The header correctly showed "Choose branch", but the nearest serving branch was never selected. The same race hit `MenuOrderTypeSync`'s explicit tab on mount. Fix: the adopt effect skips the `initial` it started from (`adoptedInitial` ref), and persist writes `cartRef.current`. Verified: a tray on F-7 Markaz (Islamabad) with a Walton, Lahore destination now moves to Walton Branch on first load.
- **Verified 2026-10-07 (header branch pill):** two `next start` builds side by side, Zaytoun (`NEXT_PUBLIC_DEFAULT_RESTAURANT=zaytoun NEXT_DIST_DIR=.next-verify`, port 3100) and Blue Hour (`.next-verify-b`, port 3101). **Pass `NEXT_PUBLIC_DEFAULT_RESTAURANT` explicitly: `.env.local`'s value changes, and it is baked in at build.** Cookies were minted locally (read-only DB queries) for four scenarios: served delivery, guest without a location, an unserved address, and pickup without a branch.
- **Bug fixed 2026-10-07: a Lahore address was served by an Islamabad branch.** "Bahria Town, Lahore" showed "Serving from Gulberg Greens" (Zaytoun, Islamabad). Root cause: coverage matched zone area names as plain text against "area, city" with NO city check, and Gulberg Greens has a (correct) zone for Islamabad's "Bahria Town" (same for Mall 1's "Gulberg"/"DHA" matching Islamabad addresses). A second hole: checkout always posts the "Delivery area" select (defaulting to the branch's FIRST zone) and `createOrder` used that zone id without checking it against the address. **Rule now (one place, `repositories/deliveries.ts#servingZones`, used by branch ranking, the checkout preview and `createOrder`): the city is a hard boundary — a zone serves an address only when its branch's `restaurant1s.city` equals the address city (case/space-insensitive); unknown city on either side = not served; only then area/postal code.** `createOrder` reads each zone with its branch city in the same statement, ignores a form zone id that does not serve the address, refuses a delivery address without a city, and now checks the tray's branch id is an active branch of this restaurant for every order type (`BRANCH_UNAVAILABLE`). `priceTray` no longer falls back to another branch's first zone. The menu destination requires a city (`validation/branching.ts`). No serviceable branch = no branch selected and ordering blocked ("Ordering isn't available in X yet. We currently deliver in …" — `BranchingState.deliveryCities`, derived from branches that have zones). Tests: `tests/branching.test.ts` (real Zaytoun zones), `tests/order-zone.test.ts`. **Data:** Zaytoun's Lahore branch "Mall 1" has no coordinates (distance not shown) and no zone covering Bahria Town, Lahore — so that address correctly gets "ordering unavailable" until an admin adds it to a Mall 1 zone.
- **Location asked once, on the menu; checkout only completes it (2026-10-07).** (1) `checkout-form.tsx`: with a branch AND destination from the menu (`locationLocked`), the delivery step is a read-only "Delivering to <label>" card + "Change" (opens the provider's location sheet via `useBranching().openLocation`, which re-ranks branches; the page re-renders and a `destinationKey` effect resets the address choice/street so an old address can never be sent) and only "House / flat no. and street" + "Floor, landmark, directions" (+ "Save for next time"). Area/city/postcode/pin are sent FROM the destination; the saved-address list, map, Area/City/Postal and the zone select are not shown (the server picks the serving zone). Not locked (feature off) = the old form unchanged. (2)+(3) `branching.tsx` provider: on `/menu`, delivery, no destination, once per browser session (`sessionStorage rp_where_asked_<slug>`): a signed-in customer's default saved address (with a city) is applied through `chooseDestination` (same ranking + cart rules as picking it); otherwise the location sheet opens — after `window.load` + 400 ms, because a modal sets `aria-hidden` on the page and doing it during streamed hydration was a hydration mismatch. Browser-verified on an isolated `NEXT_DIST_DIR=.next-verify next dev -p 3100` (guest prompt once per session; signed-in default applied; checkout card/fields/Change). Test-data gotcha: Zaytoun has a minimum order — a one-item test cart shows "We cannot take this order yet".

## 25. Map delivery zones: radius or drawn polygon (2026-10-07, migration 0030)

- **Schema (`0030_zone_geometry.sql`):** `delivery_zones.radius_km numeric(6,2)` and `polygon jsonb` ([latitude, longitude] points, 3..200). DB constraints: at most one of the two, radius 0–100 km, polygon array length. Additive — `select *` readers (snapshot, admin, `createOrder`) get null before it is applied, **but zone SAVES write the columns, so apply 0030 before deploying this code** (`npm run db:migrate`).
- **One rule, one place — `repositories/deliveries.ts#servingZones(zones, address, branchOf)`** (menu ranking, checkout preview, `createOrder`): (1) city hard boundary (section 23 fix), (2) with the customer's PIN: polygon zone = pin inside (`shared/geo.ts#pointInPolygon`, edge/corner counts as inside); radius zone = `haversineKm(pin, branch pin) <= radius_km` (branch without a pin = not served), (3) no pin (typed address) or a names-only zone: area names / postal codes as before — a map zone with no names serves no unpinned address. Geometry is never widened by names. Checks are defensive (`typeof radiusKm === "number"`, polygon array ≥3) so an older zone shape never reads as "has a radius".
- **The pin is the delivery point** (stored on the order; the rider goes there): menu destination pin → checkout (locked card sends it) → `placeOrder` → `createOrder`, whose single zone read now also selects `branch_latitude/branch_longitude` (no extra round trip).
- **Admin:** Delivery zones form has "Where this zone delivers": Area names / Radius / Draw on map (map modes need `NEXT_PUBLIC_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY`). `components/admin/zone-coverage-map.tsx` — core Maps JS `Circle`/editable `Polygon` (not the deprecated Drawing library), via the shared `loadGoogleMaps` exported from `location-picker.tsx`; click adds a corner (also a click ON the shape), drag a corner / mid-edge handle, Undo, Clear; `disableDoubleClickZoom` in polygon mode. **Two Maps gotchas found in the browser test:** a `Polygon` built with empty `paths` has no live path (clicks never show), and a bare empty `MVCArray` is read as a list of PATHS (each click became a path → "a.forEach is not a function") — create the path `MVCArray` yourself and pass `paths: new MVCArray([path])`. Validation (`validation/delivery-zones.ts`, same `shared/geo.ts#polygonProblem` the editor shows live): ≥3 points, edges must not cross (checked before the empty-area check — a symmetric figure-eight's signed area is 0), not degenerate, ≤150 km across; radius needs the branch's map pin (`services/delivery-zones.ts`, clear message). Choosing a mode clears the other geometry.
- **Branches:** Locations form has a map pin (`LocationPicker`, fills only empty address fields) and the branch **city is now required** (it is the delivery boundary). `web/admin.ts#getAdminMaps(restaurant)`.
- **Performance (measured):** 20 branches × 10 zones × 200-corner polygons = ~0.9 ms per `rankBranches` call (real restaurants: microseconds). Polygons never reach a customer's browser (checkout sends 4 zone fields; the menu gets branch options). Zone JSON ≈ 7.6 KB per 200-corner zone (admin only).
- **Tests:** `tests/zone-geometry.test.ts` (point-in-polygon incl. edge/corner/concave, polygon validation, radius, no-pin fallback, city boundary over a bad pin, ranking, form validation). Verified on the dev DB in a rolled-back transaction (constraints, the order's zone read, polygon/radius coverage) and in a browser on an isolated `.next-verify` dev server (draw 4 corners, undo, clear, crossing flagged, radius preview, branch pin) — nothing saved.
- **Earnings page removed (2026-10-07).** Sales Reports (section 22) covers revenue, so `/admin/earnings`, its sidebar link, `services/analytics.ts` and `repositories/analytics.ts#getDashboardStats`/`getBranchPerformance` (used only by it) were deleted. After deleting a route, a stale `.next/types/**` from the last build breaks `npm run typecheck` (`Cannot find module …/earnings/page.js`) until the next build — deleting `.next/types` is safe (type stubs only; a running `next start` never reads them).
- **A `staff`-role member can never add or change staff**, even if a `permissions.allow` override grants `staff.manage`: `services/team.ts` decides by ROLE (`permissions.ts#assignableRoles("staff")` is empty). Test: `tests/branch-access.test.ts`.

## 26. Super admin (platform role) and entitlements (2026-10-08, migrations 0031 + 0032)

**Who:** the developers who run the product. Role `super_admin` (`team_role` enum, 0031). No extra table: it is ONE `team_members` row in a "home" restaurant (keep that restaurant stable; deleting it deletes the login). Create it with `npm run db:create-staff -- --email … --name … --role super_admin --password … [--restaurant <home-slug>]`; nothing in the app can assign the role (`permissions.ts#assignableRoles` never lists it) and `repositories/team.ts#listTeamMembers` hides it from every restaurant's Staff screen.

**Existing platform login (2026-10-18):** `superadmin@techodos.com` (home restaurant `zaytoun`, created with `db:create-staff … --role super_admin --restaurant zaytoun`; same dev password as the other demo staff). Never make a seeded restaurant the home: every `db:seed:*` deletes its own tenant on re-run, which would delete the login.

**Login:** the SAME admin login (`/admin/login`). `auth-service.ts#signInStaff` tries the restaurant's own member, then a `super_admin` row anywhere. The token is anchored to the home restaurant; `authenticateStaff` widens it for a super admin to whichever restaurant the URL names (`actingMember`: restaurant-wide, owner-level permissions). The cookie is `rp_super_admin_session`, path `/` (`web/cookies.ts`), because one login serves `/super-admin` and `/admin`; a restaurant-scoped `rp_admin_session` wins when both exist. After login the form sends a super admin to `/super-admin`; the restaurant admin header has a "Platform admin" link back.

**RLS:** `app.has_permission/is_team_member/team_role` (0032) treat a user with an active `super_admin` row (`app.is_super_admin()`) as a restaurant-wide member of EVERY restaurant; a real row at the asked restaurant still wins. So admin reads work in any restaurant without touching a policy. `super_admin` permissions = owner's, in SQL (`app.role_permissions`) and TS (`ROLE_PERMISSIONS`, parity test needs a DB).

**Entitlements (`restaurants.entitlements` jsonb, default `{}` = everything allowed):** what the PLATFORM allows; `restaurants.features` stays the owner's own switches. `shared/feature-access.ts` is the one place that combines them (`resolveFeatures`): `Restaurant.features` is the EFFECTIVE value (owner AND platform) everywhere it is read (`mappers.ts#mapRestaurant`, plus the raw readers: `orders.ts`, `reservations.ts`, `notifications.ts`), so storefront, checkout, reservations and notifications obey it with no per-feature code. `Restaurant.ownerFeatures` is the stored value (what Settings edits) and `Restaurant.entitlements` the platform's. Keys: every boolean feature except `alaCarteEnabled` (a mode, not a capability), `emailNotify`/`pushNotify`, and the admin screens `kitchen|customers|payments|locations|staff`. Owner side: Settings hides checkboxes for disallowed keys and `services/restaurants.ts#updateRestaurantFeatures` keeps their stored value on save (so re-allowing restores the owner's old choice); the sidebar drops screens whose permission prefix is an off entitlement (`entitlementForPermission`: `coupons.view` → `coupons`, `kitchen.view` → `kitchen`, …); `web/session.ts#requirePermission` and `requireAdminPage` refuse/redirect the same way (a super admin is exempt). Add a new feature → add it to `restaurantFeaturesSchema` AND `FEATURE_ENTITLEMENTS`/`ENTITLEMENT_LABELS`; a new admin screen with its own switch → `PAGE_ENTITLEMENTS` and use a permission whose prefix is that key.

**Portal `/super-admin`** (`src/app/super-admin/`, guard `web/session.ts#requireSuperAdminPage`/`requireSuperAdmin`, which only reads the super cookie and re-checks the role in the DB): restaurant list; `[slug]` = entitlement checkboxes; `[slug]/website` = `websites` row (name, status, domain, theme/config/seo as JSON) + pages (create unpublished, delete, publish, home, order); `[slug]/website/[pageId]` = page fields + sections JSON with "Append section" (`defaultSection`). Logic in `services/platform.ts` (re-checks the role, checks a page belongs to the URL's restaurant, refuses to unset/unpublish/delete the home page, invalidates the storefront snapshot + admin cache); inputs in `validation/platform.ts` (JSON text validated against `themeSchema`/`websiteConfigSchema`/`sectionSchema`, but the parsed JSON itself is stored so unknown keys survive; new page slugs may not shadow `RESERVED_PAGE_SLUGS`). No raw JSON is shown anywhere: the website form is structured (colour pickers + live preview, fonts, announcement, nav/footer row lists, contact, social, SEO; `website-forms.tsx`) and page sections use `section-editor.tsx` (`FIELDS`: one field-spec list per section type, rendered by one `FieldInput`; collapsible cards, visible toggle, reorder, delete, add-section picker; `tests/section-editor.test.ts` fails if a type has no form or a spec names a field the schema lacks). New section type or field = a `sectionSchema` change plus a `FIELDS`/`HINTS` entry; shared widgets live in `form-parts.tsx`. Not built (add when asked): image upload (images are addresses with a preview), section drag-and-drop (arrows today), server-side refusal of coupon/review/etc. ACTIONS when only the entitlement (not the storefront feature) is off beyond the permission guard above, creating a restaurant from the portal.

**Payment-method entitlements (2026-10-09, no migration):** one key per `PAYMENT_METHODS` entry, `pay_<method>` (`PAYMENT_ENTITLEMENTS`, `paymentEntitlement(m)`), shown as the "Payment methods" group on `/super-admin/[slug]`. Unlike features they do NOT rewrite `Restaurant.settings`; `entitledPaymentMethods(methods, entitlements)` is applied where methods are consumed: `services/checkout.ts#getCheckoutOptions`, `repositories/orders.ts#createOrder` (server refusal), Settings → Payments (disallowed checkboxes hidden; the form state still holds their stored value, so a save keeps it and re-allowing restores it) and the Ordering card's self-cancel list. A new payment method gets its switch automatically; add its copy to `DESCRIPTIONS` in `entitlements-form.tsx`. **Online providers** work the same way: `provider_stripe`/`provider_jazzcash` (`ONLINE_PROVIDERS`, `PROVIDER_ENTITLEMENTS`, group "Online payment providers"); `effectiveOnlineProvider(provider, entitlements)` returns "none" for a disallowed gateway and is what `getCheckoutOptions` and `placeOrder` pass on; `createOrder` refuses `card_online`/`wallet` before inserting when it is "none". The owner's dropdown lists only allowed providers, plus the stored one as a disabled "(not available on your plan)" option so a save keeps it. A new gateway = add it to `ONLINE_PROVIDERS` + `ONLINE_PROVIDER_LABELS` + the settings zod enum + `DESCRIPTIONS`.

**Apply:** `npm run db:migrate` (0031 then 0032, in order; the enum value cannot be used in the transaction that adds it, hence two files) before running this code, since `entitlements` is selected everywhere. Then `db:create-staff … --role super_admin`. Tests: `tests/feature-access.test.ts` (DB-free). `tests/rbac.test.ts` (DB) now also loops `super_admin`.

**Super-admin design system (2026-10-08 redesign, presentation only).** Neutral SaaS look, scoped so no storefront or owner admin changes:
- **Tokens:** `.sa-root` in `globals.css` (the portal layout wraps `theme-root sa-root`) re-declares the shared semantic tokens (`--color-brand` indigo `#4F46E5`, canvas `#F8FAFC`, ink `#111827`, muted `#64748B`, hairline `#E2E8F0`, success/warning/danger/info, radius 8/12/16, Inter for headings AND body) plus `--sa-*` extras (primary hover/soft, subtle greys, focus ring, shadows, `--sa-container` 1120px). Shared `Button`/`Input`/`Card`/`Badge` pick them up untouched. A short UNLAYERED block (`.sa-root :is(input,select,textarea)`, headings, focus) sets control height 40px/radius/focus ring because it must beat the shared primitives' utilities; inside the portal use `!` utilities when a control must opt out (e.g. an input nested in a styled wrapper).
- **Primitives:** `components/super-admin/ui.tsx` (`PageHeader`, `Breadcrumbs`, `Panel` with native `<details>` when `collapsible`, accessible `Switch` role=switch, `SettingRow`, `StatusBadge` dot+word, `SaveBar` = dirty/pending status + Discard + "Save changes", pinned with `sticky` ONLY while dirty so it never covers content, `EmptyState`); `status.ts` (`restaurantStatusTone`/`websiteStatusTone`, deliberately NOT in the "use client" file: calling a client-module function from a server component throws "Attempted to call … from the server", which `next build` does not catch, only a render does); `form-parts.tsx` (`Field` wraps its control in the `<label>`, `ColorField` swatch+hex, compact `RowList`); `restaurant-tabs.tsx` (underlined, scrolls sideways on phones).
- **Screens:** list = client search/status filter/sort over the server's list (`restaurant-list.tsx`, Manage primary / Website secondary / Open admin tertiary); `[slug]/layout.tsx` renders breadcrumb + name + status + tabs once (`web/platform.ts#getPlatformRestaurant`, React-cached, so layout and page share one read); Features = grouped switch panels (`ENTITLEMENT_GROUPS`; `tests/section-editor.test.ts` fails if an entitlement is missing or duplicated) in CSS columns; Website = Pages panel + collapsible settings groups beside a sticky live preview built only from the edited values, SEO snippet preview; page editor = details panel (sticky on desktop) + sections, controls drop to their own row on phones.
- **Verified** with a throwaway Playwright script against `next dev -p 3100` (`NEXT_DIST_DIR=.next-verify`, Chrome channel, a locally signed super-admin cookie; GET only): every route 200 at 1440px and 390px with `scrollWidth - innerWidth = 0`. Re-use that approach for future visual checks; delete the script and `.next-verify` afterwards.

**Image upload in the page editor (2026-10-08).** Images are FILES in storage, never bytes in Postgres: `integrations/storage.ts#uploadMedia` puts the file in Supabase Storage (bucket `SUPABASE_STORAGE_BUCKET`, public, path `<restaurantId>/<uuid>.<ext>`) or `.uploads/` locally, and the database holds only the address: a `media` row (library metadata, `purpose = 'website'`, `uploaded_by` = the super admin) plus the URL inside the section JSON (`image.url`, gallery `images[].url`). Seeded demo restaurants (Zaytoun etc.) already read every image PATH from the DB (`restaurants.logo_url/cover_url`, `menu_items.image_url`, `website_pages.sections`, `media`), but those paths point at files shipped in `public/images/<slug>/`; real restaurants' uploads get Supabase URLs. Flow: `ImageUploadTile` (`components/super-admin/image-upload.tsx`, click or drop, JPG/PNG/WEBP/AVIF/GIF/SVG, 4 MB = the Server Action body limit) → `uploadWebsiteImageAction` → `services/platform.ts#uploadWebsiteImage` (super admin + restaurant check; deletes the stored file again if the row cannot be written) → the URL lands in the field and is saved with the page by the editor's own Save (the upload alone never changes the page). `PageEditor` supplies the upload function through `ImageUploadProvider`. **Bug found by the end-to-end run:** `repositories/media.ts#createMedia` had `coalesce($13,'{}')`, typed as text against the `text[]` `tags` column, so every media insert failed (`column "tags" is of type text[] but expression is of type text`); now `coalesce($13::text[], '{}'::text[])`. Verified live against Zaytoun: file reachable at its public URL (200 image/png), media row matches the field, page untouched until Save; the test file and row were deleted. **Media library picker (same day):** every image tile has "Choose from library" (empty) / "Library" (filled) which opens a Radix dialog (`MediaLibraryDialog` in `image-upload.tsx`, mounted inside `.sa-root`): search by file name/alt, filter All / Uploads / On website, select + "Use image" (or double-click), "Upload new" from inside the dialog; the current image is pre-selected and tagged "In use"; a pick fills an empty alt text from the library. Data: `services/platform.ts#listWebsiteLibrary` = this restaurant's `media` image rows (newest first) + any image object found in its pages' sections (`sectionImages`, tested) that has no media row, each URL once; `listWebsiteLibraryAction` runs only when a picker opens and `ImageUploadProvider` caches it per editor (dropped after an upload). Do not add `-translate-*` to a dialog using `animate-dialog`: its keyframes already apply `translate(-50%,-50%)`. Verified live on Zaytoun at desktop and phone widths (10 images; pick changes the field and dirties the save bar; nothing saved). Not built: removing an image from a section does not delete the stored file (other sections may use it).

## 27. Restaurant admin redesign, direction A (2026-10-08)

Approved by the user from rendered concepts (three directions, real DB themes and Zaytoun data). **Frozen on purpose: the dashboard screen (`(dashboard)/page.tsx`, incl. the dark "Service" hero and status tiles) and the dark sidebar/header colours** — do not restyle them. The theme is each restaurant's own `websites.theme` (fallback `restaurants.primary_color`), applied as before by `getAdminTheme` → `themeCssVariables`; nothing here hard-codes a colour: brand for actions, the derived `--color-success|warning|danger|info` for states.
- **Primitives:** `components/admin/admin-ui.tsx` (`StatusPill` dot+word, `orderStatusTone`, `reservationStatusTone`, `badgeTone` for lists that already pick a `Badge` variant, `AdminSection`, `tableHead`/`tableRow` classes), `admin-switch.tsx` (`AdminSwitch` role=switch in brand colour, `AdminSwitchRow`), `list-filter.tsx` (`ListFilter`: instant client search over server-rendered rows with `data-filter-text`; a MutationObserver re-applies it after a server refresh), `order-next-step-button.tsx`. `AdminStatusTabs` is now a segmented control; `AdminPagination` takes optional `total`/`pageSize` ("Showing 21–40 of 63"). The dashboard still uses `Badge` + `orderStatusBadgeVariant` + `AdminEmptyState`, so those three are unchanged.
- **Orders:** table on wide laptops (`xl`+), cards below `xl` (see section 19, 2026-10-19); each active order has a one-click next step (`shared/order-flow.ts#nextOrderStep`: confirm → start preparing → mark ready → out for delivery [delivery only] → completed; tested in `tests/order-flow.test.ts`) through the same `updateOrderStatusAction` (DB transition rules + notifications unchanged; no client refresh, the action revalidates `/orders`).
- **Menu:** categories rail (`CategoryManager` now takes `baseHref` STRING + `activeId`, `canManage`; a server page cannot pass a function to a client component — `hrefFor` crashed with "Functions cannot be passed directly to Client Components"), items list with photo, price, Featured/Buffet pills, availability switch (`ItemRowActions`/`BranchItemToggle`, same actions), Edit, and `ListFilter` search.
- **Reservations:** grouped by day (order of the list kept), one row per booking; `ReservationStatusControl` unchanged apart from an aria-label. **Settings:** sticky jump-link nav (`#settings-<section>`), single column; every `SectionForm` still saves on its own, now with "Unsaved changes" + "Save changes" enabled only when that section changed; booleans render as `AdminSwitch`; Features are switch rows with a line each. Other screens (order detail, kitchen, customers, coupons, reviews, zones, locations, payments, reports, staff) got the shared pills/table header/tabs.
- The header's super-admin "Platform admin" link is `hidden xl:inline-flex` (it pushed the branch picker off phones and overflowed tablets).
- **Header layout once a location is set (desktop, 2026-10-11, owner's request):**
  - **Left:** a ☰ button before the logo opens the menu sheet from the left (`Sheet side="left"`; phones keep the bottom sheet and the right-hand ☰).
  - **Right:** branch pill → cart → profile. My Orders moves out of the header into that sheet at `lg`.
  - **Profile:** signed in, it shows `AccountInitial`: the capital first letter in a 28px disc of `--color-brand` lifted 18% toward white, with the letter in the brand foreground tinted toward the brand. The button is `px-1.5` / hover `pl-1.5 pr-4`, so the disc sits 6px in from every edge of the 40px pill. The name slides open on hover.
  - **Left ☰:** `MenuButton flip` puts the short bar on the left (it faces the logo); the phone ☰ on the right keeps it on the right.
  - **Menu sheet:** lists the configured nav plus every other live page the layout passes as `extraLinks`: Book a Table (reservations on), Reviews (`features.reviews`), Locations (any location). Links are de-duplicated by href, so Blue Hour's "Cafés" (= /locations) hides "Locations".
  - **Unchanged:** the centre nav stays in the header, and with no location set the header looks as before.
- **Header refinement (2026-10-11):**
  - **Header:** no drop shadow, only the hairline `border-b`.
  - **Action icons (`headerIconLinkClass`):** no hover border. Hover/focus is a `--tint-strong` fill with the same slide-open label, and keyboard focus gets an accent outline.
  - **Desktop branch pill:** unchanged; the owner wants its original styling and sizes (2026-10-11).
  - **Logos:** `public/images/zaytoun/logo.svg` lost its baked-in `#1B2B24` `<rect>` plate (viewBox unchanged). Its cream wordmark now needs a dark ground, which is true of Zaytoun's dark header tone; it would vanish if `navigation.tone` were switched to `light`. Check other DB logos for the same baked-in plate.
- **Verified** with Playwright against `next dev -p 3100` (`NEXT_DIST_DIR=.next-verify`, Chrome channel, locally signed super-admin cookie, GET only) at 1440/820/390 px: every admin route 200, `scrollWidth - innerWidth = 0`. To see another restaurant's admin locally, start that dev instance with `NEXT_PUBLIC_DEFAULT_RESTAURANT=<slug>`: the admin layout's `getLocations` reads the storefront snapshot, which serves ONE restaurant per instance (section 15), so other slugs 500 with "Restaurant not found" on the same instance — pre-existing, and it also affects the super admin's "Open admin" for restaurants other than the instance's.
- **Shell update (2026-10-09, user request, colours unchanged):** header = breadcrumb on the left (`admin-sidebar.tsx#AdminBreadcrumb`, from `NAV_GROUPS`: "Group › Page › sub-page", sub-pages named by `subPageLabel`), branch chip (`BranchSwitcher`/`BranchLabel` restyled) and the sound toggle (now a volume icon; it IS the only bell there was — no notification centre exists, none was invented) on the right; height `h-14`, same as the sidebar's brand block. Sidebar: "Dashboard" label, "Sales reports", an active-orders count on Orders (layout runs `getOrderStatusCounts` for the branch in scope — one statement per admin page view), and a user card at the foot (avatar, name, role) whose Radix dropdown holds Platform admin (super admin) and Sign out (moved out of the header). The drawer on phones shows the same user card.
- **Header branch pill hides until chosen (2026-10-09, owner's request):** `header-branch-pill.tsx` renders NOTHING while the gate is `need-location`/`need-branch` (it used to show "Select location"/"Choose branch"); the location and branch are asked at "Add to cart" (quick-add, item customizer and the cart call `branching.resolveGate`), and the pill appears with the chosen branch afterwards, as before. The desktop hairline before it moved into the component (`divider` prop) so it hides with the pill. Unchanged: the menu page still opens the location sheet on arrival when nothing is chosen.
- **Staff created from the admin could not sign in (fixed 2026-10-09).** A brand-new staff email added through the Staff screen on hosted Supabase gets its login from the Supabase Auth Admin API (`services/team.ts`), and Supabase stores that password as **bcrypt** (`$2a$10$…`), while `server/auth/password.ts#verifyPassword` only understood the app's own `scrypt$…` format, so every such member got "Invalid email or password" (4 accounts at the time, e.g. Zaytoun's Walton Branch staff). **Same day, unified to ONE format, bcrypt:** `server/auth/password.ts` now hashes AND verifies bcrypt only (`bcryptjs`, exact-pinned, cost 10 like Supabase, the password as typed with no NFKC, like Supabase), so a login made by `create-staff`, any seed, customer sign-up/reset or the Staff screen (Supabase Auth Admin API) is the same format; `scrypt$…` hashes are refused. bcrypt reads only 72 bytes, so `fitsPasswordLimit` caps passwords in `validation/customer-auth.ts`, `validation/team.ts` and `create-staff.ts` (refused, never cut). Test: `tests/password.test.ts`. **All logins and dummy people data were deleted at the owner's request (2026-10-09, one transaction):** every `auth.users` row (38) and `team_members` row (32, incl. the super admin and all owners), every customer (32, with addresses/push tokens/codes), order (51, with items/payments/deliveries/history), reservation (36), review (48), notification event and legacy DB cart. Restaurants, menus, branches, zones, coupons, websites and media were kept. Nobody can sign in until logins are recreated: `npm run db:create-staff -- --email … --name … --role super_admin --password … --restaurant <slug>` (then owners per restaurant the same way, or from the super admin's "Open admin" → Staff). Re-running a seed recreates its demo people (now bcrypt) AND its demo orders/reviews.

## 28. Production audit: security, integrity, load testing and capacity (2026-10-09)

Full audit; every fix is verified by a test against a REAL local Postgres (not only fakes). Migrations **0033, 0034, 0035, 0036** were written and applied only to local throwaway databases: **apply them (`npm run db:migrate`) before deploying this code** (0033 is required: `createOrder` writes `orders.idempotency_key`).

### Security / integrity fixes (each has a regression test)
- **Account takeover through sign-up (critical).** `createCustomerAccount` and `createGoogleCustomer` used `on conflict (restaurant_id, phone) do update`, so signing up with SOMEONE ELSE'S (never verified) phone number rewrote their account's email + password (or Google link) and returned a session for it. The conflict branch now only upgrades a GUEST row with no email or the same email (`where customers.is_guest and ...`); otherwise CONFLICT "An account with that phone number already exists". Linking Google onto an account whose email was never verified clears that password (`linkGoogleToCustomer`), so someone who pre-registered another person's email cannot share the account. `tests/customer-account-takeover.test.ts` (reproduced first: 4 of 6 failed before the fix).
- **Super admin wrote into the wrong restaurant.** Admin Server Actions call `requirePermission(p)` without a slug and `authenticateStaff` then checked the member's HOME restaurant: a super admin working in restaurant B's admin created categories/coupons/zones/items in restaurant A. `web/session.ts#getCurrentStaff` now defaults the slug from the trusted `x-rp-pathname` header the middleware sets on every page request; since section 37 a path under `/admin` (`shared/utils.ts#isAdminPath`) maps to this deployment's restaurant (`web/instance.ts`); the slug only narrows which membership is checked, so it cannot widen access. Test: `tests/admin-orders.test.ts`.
- **Stored XSS via JSON-LD.** `JsonLd` used `JSON.stringify` inside `<script>`; approved review author/title/comment (typed by anonymous visitors) are in the reviews page JSON-LD, so `</script><script>…` executed for every visitor. `shared/utils.ts#scriptSafeJson` escapes `<` and U+2028/2029. `tests/json-ld.test.ts`.
- **Security headers** (`next.config.ts#headers`): `X-Frame-Options: SAMEORIGIN` + CSP `frame-ancestors 'self'` (admin/checkout clickjacking), `nosniff`, `Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin` (order links carry `?t=` tokens), `Permissions-Policy` (geolocation self only), HSTS (ignored on plain http, so local dev is unaffected).
- **Rate limits**: per-ACCOUNT sign-in limits for staff (10 / 15 min per email) and customers (10 / 15 min per restaurant + email) on top of the per-IP ones (the IP key alone let one account be guessed from many addresses); customer per-IP sign-in raised 8 → 30 per 5 min (mobile carriers put many customers behind one IP); review submission 5 / 10 min per IP; promo-code checks 30 / 5 min per IP; the limiter's map is bounded (oldest dropped when full). `finishGoogleSignupAction` validates the phone (`finishGoogleSignupSchema`, E.164); it stored any string.
- **Duplicate orders on retry (0033).** There was no idempotency: a network retry, a resubmit after "We could not confirm your order", or two racing requests created two orders. The checkout form sends one random `idempotencyKey` per checkout; `createOrder` returns the order this customer already placed with that key (`replayed: true`, found by the SAME single read, no extra round trip), and the unique index `orders_idempotency_key_uidx (restaurant_id, customer_id, idempotency_key)` makes two concurrent requests safe (the loser's insert fails with 23505 and runs once more, finding the winner's order). A replayed online-payment order gets the same Stripe session (Stripe idempotency key `checkout-<orderId>`) and no new intent once paid. `tests/order-idempotency.test.ts` (10 simultaneous submits → 1 order).
- **Coupon usage-limit race.** The limit was checked against an unlocked read, so simultaneous orders all passed it (12 of 12 redeemed a 3-use code in the test). The usage increment is now `update coupons … where usage_limit is null or used_count < usage_limit returning id` inside the order's write statement; no row counted → `COUPON_USAGE_LIMIT` and the whole order rolls back.
- **Kitchen tickets lost their add-ons (0034).** `order_item_addons` has RLS with only customer/guest policies; every staff read (order detail, kitchen, lists) runs as `app_runtime`, so each line came back without its add-ons ("Margherita" instead of stuffed crust + extra mozzarella). 0034 adds the team select policy (then rewritten by 0035).
- **Orders into a closed kitchen.** Opening hours were only checked when the checkout PAGE rendered; a page left open past closing time (or a direct request) still ordered. `createOrder` refuses (`ORDERING_DISABLED`) when the branch that cooks it (tray branch, else the delivery zone's branch, else the primary branch) is closed; its hours come from the same single read. `CreateOrderInput.now` pins the clock (DB tests use `BELLA_OPEN_NOW` from `tests/helpers/db.ts`). The checkout page now evaluates the cart's branch, not always the primary one.
- **Reservation double-booking** fixed with a per-branch-per-day advisory lock (section 17 note).
- **Reservation timezone bugs.** `assertBookable` parsed `new Date("<date>T<time>:00")` in the SERVER's zone (UTC on Vercel): a 19:00 Karachi booking was checked as midnight and refused as closed. The `/reservation` page probed every slot as `…Z` (UTC), hiding Karachi evening slots from 19:00 on any server. `shared/hours.ts#zonedDateTime(date, time, tz)` (restaurant wall clock → instant, DST-safe) is used by both and by `isSlotInPast` (which ignored its timezone argument).
- **CSV formula injection**: the sales CSV export wrote customer names as typed, so a name like `=HYPERLINK(...)` ran as a formula when staff opened it in Excel. `shared/reports.ts#csvText` prefixes `=` `+` `-` `@` tab/CR cells with `'` (OWASP). The XLSX export stores strings as text and was already safe. `tests/reports-csv.test.ts`.
- **Admin activity-poll index (0036)**: `orders_restaurant_updated_idx (restaurant_id, updated_at)`; the 7 s poll read every order of the restaurant (581 blocks / 4.3 ms at 3,200 orders, linear), now 2 blocks / 0.06 ms.
- **Payment confirmation**: a verified success now also resolves a payment marked `failed` (a failed first attempt or gateway hiccup followed by a real payment left a paid order showing "failed"); a failure never overwrites a paid one. `tests/payment-confirmation.test.ts`.
- **DB layer**: a transaction whose rollback fails (connection gone) now discards the client (`client.release(true)`) instead of returning a broken connection to the pool.
- **Fresh databases could not be migrated.** `0019_replace_users_table.sql` drops a constraint by the name it had on the hosted DB only, so a database built from these files failed there. `scripts/db/migrate.mjs` runs `db/migrations/compat/<file>.pre.sql` (same transaction) only while `<file>` is pending; applied databases and checksums are untouched. Verified: every migration applies to an empty database.

### Performance fix: staff RLS once per statement (0035)
Every staff read evaluated `app.has_permission(restaurant_id, …)` and `app.can_access_location(…)` PER ROW (SECURITY DEFINER SQL functions, never inlined, about 4 sub-queries per row). The admin layout's status count took 181 ms for 1,451 orders (linear: seconds at 100k) on every admin page view, plus the kitchen, lists and reports. 0035 adds `app.permitted_restaurant_ids(p)`, `app.branch_scoped_restaurant_ids()` and `app.member_location_ids()` (arrays computed once per statement in an InitPlan) and rewrites the team policies of orders, order_items, order_item_addons, order_status_history, payments, deliveries, reservations, reviews and customers as array membership tests. **Same rows:** a differential check (every staff login × every pinned restaurant × all 9 tables, old vs new policies in one rolled-back transaction) found 0 differences on two databases, and the full suite (RBAC parity, tenant and branch isolation) passes. 181 ms → 2.3 ms; admin orders page 316 → 91 ms, kitchen 263 → 99 ms, dashboard 362 → 58 ms, status change 364 → 89 ms. Rule for new policies on big tables: never call a per-row SECURITY DEFINER function; use `col = any (coalesce((select app.permitted_restaurant_ids('x.view')), '{}'))`.

### Local Postgres (DB suites + load tests; never the hosted DB)
No admin rights needed. In any scratch folder: `npm i embedded-postgres@17.10.0-beta.17`, then with its `native/bin`: `initdb -D data -U postgres --pwfile=<file containing postgres> -E UTF8 --locale=C -A scram-sha-256` (UTF8 matters: the default WIN1252 cluster rejects the seed data) and `pg_ctl -D data -l pg.log -o "-p 54329 -c max_connections=400" start` (use `pg_ctl`, not `postgres.exe`: Postgres refuses to run as an administrator except through pg_ctl's restricted token). Then run `scripts/db/bootstrap.sql` as `postgres`, plus `create role service_role nologin bypassrls` (Supabase's role; some migrations grant to it) and `create database <name> owner app_owner`; `DATABASE_URL_MIGRATOR=postgresql://app_owner:app_owner@localhost:54329/<name> node scripts/db/migrate.mjs`; the seeds with `DATABASE_URL_MIGRATOR` and `DATABASE_URL` set to the same URL (seeds read `.env.local` only for keys NOT already set). Run the DB suites with `DATABASE_URL_TEST=postgresql://app_runtime:app_runtime@localhost:54329/<name>` and `DATABASE_URL_TEST_SERVICE=…app_service:app_service…`. Result on a fresh database: 57 files / 562 tests pass. Opening-hours JSON keys are `sun`…`sat` (not `monday`); a wrong key means "no hours", which reads as closed.

**Resuming a cluster left over from an earlier session (2026-10-14):** the `embedded-postgres` npm package's own `.start()` sometimes rejects with a bare `undefined` (no message) on a cluster that wasn't shut down cleanly — a stale `data/postmaster.pid` naming a PID that's no longer running is the usual cause; delete that one file and retry, or skip the JS wrapper entirely and start the cluster directly with its own bundled binary: `./node_modules/@embedded-postgres/<platform>/native/bin/pg_ctl.exe -D data -l pg.log -o "-p 54329" start` (same binary the wrapper calls internally, but its own error output is real). Databases and roles created in a previous session persist on disk (`data/`) — check `select datname from pg_database` before re-running `bootstrap.sql`/`create database`, and run only the migrations newer than what's already applied (`npm run db:migrate` against it is idempotent and safe either way, section 11's `scripts/db/migrate.mjs` only applies what's missing).

### Load testing (scripts/load, k6)
Isolation: production builds into `NEXT_DIST_DIR=.next-load-<slug>` (one build per restaurant: `NEXT_PUBLIC_DEFAULT_RESTAURANT` is baked in), started with every credential BLANKED in the environment (Next applies `.env.local` only to keys that are undefined, and the config treats `""` as not configured), so email, push, Stripe, JazzCash, Google and Supabase storage are off and `DATABASE_URL`, `DATABASE_URL_SERVICE`, `DATABASE_URL_LISTEN` point at the local DB. A `next build` into a new folder adds `.<folder>/types/**` to `tsconfig.json`'s `include`; harmless since `exclude: [".next-*"]` (section 11). Do NOT `git checkout -- tsconfig.json` to undo it: that also throws away any uncommitted tsconfig change (it did, once, 2026-10-12); remove the added line by hand instead. Steps: `LOADTEST_DB_URL=… LOADTEST_AUTH_SECRET=<the servers' AUTH_SECRET> npx tsx scripts/load/prep.ts` (1,100 + 400 verified test customers with signed sessions, cart cookies, owner staff sessions, branches open 24h) → `node scripts/load/actions.mjs bella-napoli=.next-load-bella zaytoun=.next-load-zaytoun` (Server Action ids differ per build) → `k6 run -e PROFILE=ramp|spike|soak|baseline|smoke -e PEAK=1000 -e THINK=2.5 -e BELLA_URLS=http://localhost:3201,… scripts/load/load.js` (k6 v1.1.0, single binary from its GitHub releases) with `node scripts/load/monitor.mjs` alongside, then `node scripts/load/verify.mjs <start ISO>` (no lost or duplicate orders; totals, payments, history, deliveries, outbox). `scripts/load/capacity.js` is an open-model arrival-rate probe for ONE process. Server Actions are invoked over HTTP exactly like the browser: `POST <page path>` with `Next-Action: <id>`, `Content-Type: text/plain;charset=UTF-8`, body = JSON array of the arguments; the result is the RSC line containing `"success"`.

Scenario mix: 70% browsers (home → menu → item → cart, plus reviews/locations; half signed in), 30% buyers (menu → promo check → cart → checkout → place order → order page → current orders; half delivery), 10% of checkouts double-submitted at once, 25% of users on a second restaurant (Zaytoun, its own process), 6 admins polling activity every 7 s and confirming new orders while opening kitchen/orders/dashboard. Think time ×2.5 ≈ one page every 5–15 s per user.

Results on the audit machine (laptop i5-8365U, 4 cores / 8 threads, 16 GB; k6, Postgres 17 and the Node servers all on it; 3 Bella + 1 Zaytoun `next start` processes, DB_POOL_MAX 10):
- **Correctness under 1,006 concurrent users: no lost, duplicated or inconsistent order in any run.** Run A 1,586 orders and Run B 1,650 orders = exactly the rows in the DB; 143 / 156 simultaneous double-submits all answered with the same order; every order has lines, one payment equal to its total, history, and a delivery row when delivery; the outbox was fully processed; page errors 0.00% / 0.01%.
- **Speed at 1,006 users is CPU-bound on this machine.** Run A (before 0035, gzip on): 44 req/s, storefront p95 16.4 s. Run B (0035, `NEXT_COMPRESS=false`): 57.7 req/s, storefront median 1.2 s, p95 7.8 s, order p95 11 s. The four Node processes sat at ~100–117% CPU while Postgres idled (≤ 7 active connections, 0–2 lock waits). Neither the database nor the pool is the bottleneck; React/Next rendering is (CPU profile: framework render and serialisation; application code ~3–4% of ticks).
- **Spike (Run C: 50 → 1,000 users in 10 s, 3 min, drop, 2 min recovery):** no crash; 666 orders reported = 666 in the DB; 3 orders failed client-side with connection resets during the burst and left NO row (a retry with the same key would place them once); page errors 0.03%; CPU back to idle within ~30 s of the drop.
- **Soak (Run D: 300 users, 12 min):** storefront p95 113 ms / p99 204 ms, order p95 134 ms, 0 errors in 30,618 requests, 1,227 orders all consistent, 130 double-submits deduplicated; memory flat at ~500–620 MB per process after warm-up (no leak trend). This machine serves ~300 concurrent users comfortably.
- **One process (open model, storefront mix):** p95 0.2 s at 24 req/s with Next's gzip, 0.15 s at 30 req/s without it; collapse at ~36 req/s. Next's own gzip costs 5–15 ms CPU per page, so `NEXT_COMPRESS=false` behind a compressing proxy/CDN gives ~20–25% more capacity.
- **Sizing:** 1,000 concurrent users at this think time ask for ~100–120 page requests/s (+ ~2.5 orders/s). Plan at least one Node process per ~25 req/s at p95 < 0.3 s on a core like this one: **5–6 processes on dedicated cores per busy restaurant** (PM2 cluster or several containers behind a load balancer), or Vercel autoscaling. On Vercel each new instance loads the storefront snapshot (section 15) before it answers, so pre-warm before a known spike. Re-measure on the real host with these scripts; a server-class core is usually faster than this laptop's. Not tested: the real Supabase pooler latency (each extra round trip there costs ~1–300 ms), Stripe/JazzCash/Resend/FCM under load (unconfigured by design), and more than 4 app processes sharing one database.

### Navigation feedback: route skeletons + admin progress bar (2026-10-10)
Symptom: clicking a storefront link (Reservations most visibly) did nothing for 1–5 s, then the page jumped in. Cause: App Router keeps the old page on screen until the new one has rendered on the server, and only shows something sooner when the target segment has a `loading.tsx` (prefetched with the link). Only menu/cart/checkout/orders/current-orders had one; reservation does database reads (booked slots + profile, ~0.3–1 s per round trip on the hosted pooler); nothing gave click feedback.
- Every storefront route now has its own `loading.tsx` shaped like the page: reservation, reviews, locations, `[pageSlug]`, `menu/[itemSlug]`, `order/[orderNumber]`, `account/*`, and home. Shared parts: `components/storefront/page-skeletons.tsx` (`PageHeroSkeleton` runs under the transparent header like `PageHero`, `ReservationFormSkeleton`, `SectionBlocksSkeleton`). **The home page lives in the `(site)/(home)` route group** so its skeleton is only the home page's: a `loading.tsx` beside `(site)/layout.tsx` would also stand in for every page without its own. The dish sheet (`@modal/(.)menu/[itemSlug]`) has none on purpose: it is served from the snapshot and a skeleton would make its sheet animate twice.
- `/reservation` streams: the photo panel (no database) renders at once and `ReservationFormSection` (availability + profile) is behind `<Suspense fallback={<ReservationFormSkeleton />}>`. Fixed in the same file: the phone link used `replace(/s+/g, "")` (removed the letter s) instead of `/\s+/`.
- Admin only: `components/admin/navigation-progress.tsx` (`NavigationProgress`, mounted in `(dashboard)/layout.tsx` inside `Suspense`): a 3 px bar in `--color-brand-accent` that starts on a click on an internal link to another URL, a GET form submit, or back/forward, and finishes when the pathname/search changes (15 s safety timeout). No dependency; the storefront deliberately has no bar.
- Measured on a production build with a proxy adding 300 ms per database reply: menu → reservation responds in 167 ms (skeleton; complete 5.1 s), admin orders → kitchen 122 ms (bar; complete ~1 s); no console errors. After moving a page, a stale `.next/types` breaks `tsc` until the next build; delete `.next/types`.

### Admin filter pills (`AdminStatusTabs`) didn't show loading feedback on search-param-only navigation (2026-10-17)
Symptom: in admin reports (also orders, reviews, reservations, payments — same list-page shape), clicking a filter pill like "Yesterday" appeared to do nothing — the page looked stuck until the new data silently swapped in. Cause: each of those pages is one big `async` Server Component that fetches everything before returning JSX, and `loading.tsx` **only fires when React first suspends the route segment** (hard navigation / first paint). Next.js does not re-trigger it for a Link navigation that keeps the same route and only changes `searchParams` — React reuses the already-resolved boundary instead of re-suspending, so the existing `loading.tsx` for all five pages was dead code for this exact case (the one time users actually use these filters).
- Fix: split each page into (a) a thin outer Server Component that awaits only auth/restaurant/scope (fast, not filter-dependent) and (b) an inner `async` component holding the filter-dependent query + its rendering, wrapped in `<Suspense key={...} fallback={...}>` where the key is built from every searchParam that affects the query (status/q/page, or preset/from/to, plus `locationId`). Changing the key forces React to unmount/resuspend on every filter change, so the fallback (the same skeleton pieces `loading.tsx` already used, from `components/admin/admin-skeleton.tsx`) shows every time, not just on first load.
- Applied to `reports/page.tsx` (`ReportsResults`), `orders/page.tsx` (`OrdersResults`), `reviews/page.tsx` (`ReviewsResults`), `reservations/page.tsx` (`ReservationsResults`), `payments/page.tsx` (`PaymentsResults`). Header title/description that depends on the query result (e.g. "`N orders`") moved inside the Suspense boundary too, since it can't be known before the fetch resolves — only the parts genuinely independent of filters (branch-scope status-count fetch for the orders tabs, for example) stay outside.
- **Rule for any future admin list page with `AdminStatusTabs`/`AdminPagination`/a search `Form`:** don't rely on route-level `loading.tsx` alone for same-page filter changes — wrap the filtered section in its own keyed `Suspense`, same pattern as above.

### Branch opening hours: set in admin, "not set" ≠ closed, closed checkout button (2026-10-10)
- **Bug (reported on Zaytoun "Mall 1 · Lahore"):** checkout said "The kitchen is closed right now" while the footer said "Open 24 hours". The footer shows the PRIMARY branch (F-7, 24 h); the cart's branch Mall 1 (and Walton) had `hours = {}` because the admin Locations form had no hours field, so every branch added there had none — and `{}` evaluated as closed forever. It surfaced once checkout/createOrder started checking the cart's own branch (section 28).
- **Rule now (`shared/hours.ts#acceptsOrdersAt`):** for ORDERING, a branch with no hours set takes orders at any time; hours that are set are enforced (24 h = open === close; close < open runs past midnight). Used by `services/cart.ts#serviceAvailability` and `createOrder`. Reservations keep `isOpenAt` (no hours = no slots).
- **Admin Locations form has an "Opening hours" editor** (`components/admin/opening-hours-editor.tsx`): "not set" state (with "Open 24 hours" / "Set opening hours"), per day on/off, "24 hours", up to 3 windows (split lunch/dinner), "Copy to all days", "Clear hours". Validated by `validation/locations.ts#openingHoursSchema` (day keys `sun`…`sat`, `HH:MM`, ≤ 3 windows; every day closed is refused — it would be stored as `{}` and read as always open; mark the branch inactive instead). The branch list shows a one-line summary. `repositories/restaurants.ts` already saved `hours` on create/update (omitted = keep).
- **The admin Locations page reads branches from the DATABASE** (`services/restaurants.ts#getLocationsForAdmin`, as the staff member so inactive branches show), not the storefront snapshot: right after a save the snapshot is still reloading, so the list showed old data (found by the browser test).
- **Checkout order summary, compact (2026-10-12):**
  - **Item list:** capped at every width (`max-h-[17.5rem]`, desktop `min(34vh,17.5rem)`), scrolls inside the card (`overscroll-contain`, focusable when there are more than 4 lines). The height leaves the next row peeking out, so a long list reads as scrollable. Checked with 11 dishes: desktop Place order ends at 895 px in a 1000 px window; on a 375 px phone there is no overflow.
  - **Rows:** `[44px thumb] [name (2 lines max) / "Qty n · variant · add-ons"] … [line total]`.
  - **Header:** title + item count only. The "Your delicious meal…" line was removed.
  - **Total:** shown once, in the Total due block (`whitespace-nowrap`, 1.35rem on phones). The desktop Place order button no longer repeats it: `placeOrderContent(withTotal)`. The phones' sticky bar keeps the amount, because the summary is off-screen there.
  - **`ctaButton`** is `h-13`.
- **Closed = disabled button, not a blocking page:** `services/cart.ts#branchClosedLabel` → "Restaurant closed · opens 12pm" (single branch) / "Branch closed · opens 4am" (multi-branch). The cart's Checkout button (`LocalCartPanel` `closedBranches`, computed per active branch in `cart-view.tsx`; the cart's branch, else the primary) and checkout's Place order (`CheckoutForm` `closedLabel`; the form still renders) show only that label, disabled. Other blockers (minimum order, unavailable items, ordering switched off) keep the "We cannot take this order yet" page. createOrder still refuses regardless.
- Verified in a browser on an isolated build at 390/1280 px; tests: `tests/opening-hours.test.ts`, `tests/create-order-sql.test.ts`. Hosted data: Zaytoun's Mall 1 and Walton Branch have no hours (they now take orders at any time); set their real hours in admin → Locations.

### Admin success toast in step with the screen (2026-10-10)
Reported: "Order … marked confirmed" appeared while the Confirm button still spun and the row still read Pending. An admin action that revalidates resolves when the WRITE is done, but its transition stays pending until Next has applied the page re-rendered into the same response (seconds on the hosted DB), so toasting on the result ran ahead of the screen. `components/admin/use-settled-toast.ts#useSettledToast(pending)` queues the success message and shows it when the transition settles (or when the control unmounts because the row left the filter); the controls await the action inside an async `startTransition`. Used by `OrderNextStepButton`, `OrderStatusControl`, `ReservationStatusControl`, `ReviewModerationControl` (which also lost a redundant `router.refresh()` after its revalidating action: a second full render per click). Errors still toast at once. Measured behind a 300 ms-per-round-trip DB proxy: toast, spinner end and row change within 35 ms of each other (~4 s after the click; the wait itself is the page re-render).

### Manual payment status for offline payments (2026-10-10)
Before this, nothing in the admin could change a payment: only the Stripe return/webhook and the JazzCash return did (`confirmOnlinePayment`), so every cash / cash-on-delivery / card-terminal / bank-transfer payment stayed Pending forever. Now staff with `orders.manage` (owner, admin, manager; not staff) can set it from the order detail page (under the payment line) and from each row of the Payments page (`components/admin/payment-status-control.tsx`).
- Rule, one place: `shared/payment-flow.ts#manualPaymentTargets(method, status)` — only methods other than `card_online`/`wallet` (those belong to their gateway); pending → paid | failed, failed → paid | pending ("Mark unpaid"), paid → refunded | pending; nothing from authorized/refunded/cancelled. "Mark refunded" and "Mark unpaid" ask for confirmation.
