**Branding is data, never hardcoded** (a test greps the templates for restaurant names, PKR/Rs and seed colours): `restaurants.primary_color` → `emailPalette()` derives the button text (black/white by contrast), an accent darkened to ≥ 4.5:1 on white, and brand-tinted canvas/panel/rules; a pale brand gets a white header with an accent wordmark, a dark one a filled band. Invalid colour → neutral `#7a5c3e`. Logo (`emailLogoUrl`): used only if it is an absolute http(s) raster image — a relative path is made absolute with `NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL` (`brand.siteUrl`, passed by both senders), SVG is dropped (Gmail/Outlook do not render it) — otherwise the restaurant name as a serif wordmark (also what shows with images blocked). Footer: name, tel/mailto links, site host.

**Codes:** shown exactly as generated (one text node, spacing is letter-spacing, never in a URL); expiry is the real `CODE_TTL_MINUTES` (`expiresInMinutes`), no longer the hardcoded "10 minutes" string; subject `"<code> is your <restaurant> verification|password reset code"`; "never share this code" in both.

**Tests:** `tests/email-templates.test.ts` (all six for two brands — own name/colour only, no `undefined`/`null`/`NaN`/`${`, balanced tags, contrast for light/dark/edge colours, code + expiry exactness, logo rules, currency/amounts/charges, escaping and very long values), `tests/auth-email-flow.test.ts` (real DB, faked provider: code read back from the rendered email verifies; wrong / expired / replaced codes refused; full reset then sign-in with the new password; unknown email gets nothing). Browser screenshots of all 12 renders at 680 and 375 px: no horizontal overflow. **Not tested:** real inbox rendering (Gmail, Outlook, Apple Mail) — no Resend key or Litmus-type service here; send one of each through a configured Resend account before launch.

## Demo / marketing video (scripts/demo-video)

Playwright + DevTools-screencast recorder of the REAL site (customer order → admin confirms → kitchen board → customer
sees "On the way" → menu/reports/payments/locations tour), FFmpeg assembly, original generated music. Full steps in
`scripts/demo-video/README.md`. Gotchas learned 2026-10-17: run it against an isolated `next start -p 3100` build with
`RESEND_API_KEY`/`FCM_*` blank so status changes notify nobody (`next build` with `NEXT_DIST_DIR` rewrites `tsconfig.json` —
`git checkout tsconfig.json`); guest checkout needs an emailed code, so record with a signed-in customer; a saved account
phone is locked at checkout, so mask it on screen with `DEMO_REDACT` (display-only, data untouched); headless screencast
only emits frames on change, so the recorder forces a repaint on every cut (`live()`) and animates cards only after capture
resumes; cursor/scroll motion must be time-based (per-step CDP latency stretched 1 s glides to 5 s). Observed: the cart
said "Ordering from Mall 1" but the order was filed under Walton Branch (main) after the address was typed — worth checking.

## 40. WhatsApp checkout (phase 1, 2026-10-18, migration 0043)

For small restaurants that never open the admin: the customer checks out on the website and the order is handed to
the restaurant's WhatsApp. **Mode is platform-set**: `restaurants.checkout_mode` `'standard'|'whatsapp'` (default standard)
in super admin → Features → **Checkout** panel (`checkout-mode-form.tsx` → `saveCheckoutModeAction` →
`services/platform.ts#saveCheckoutMode`, refuses WhatsApp without a usable number). **Number**: `restaurants.whatsapp`
(admin → Settings → **WhatsApp ordering**, `whatsapp-settings-section.tsx` → `updateWhatsAppNumberAction` →
`services/restaurants.ts#updateRestaurantWhatsApp`; clearing it is refused while the mode is whatsapp) with an optional
per-branch override `restaurant1s.whatsapp` (Locations form). All three forms normalise via
`shared/whatsapp-order.ts#normalizeWhatsAppNumber` ("0300 1234567" → "+923001234567"; `validation/common.ts#whatsappNumberField`).
**Flow**: checkout form (`checkoutMode` prop) skips the email OTP, email optional, button "Send order on WhatsApp";
`placeOrder` refuses before writing if `resolveWhatsAppNumber` (branch → restaurant) finds none, standard mode now
requires email in the service (the schema made it optional); `createOrder` (the boundary) reads `checkout_mode`: cash
methods only (`WHATSAPP_PAYMENT_METHODS` = cash_on_delivery/cash, regardless of the owner's Payments settings, still
entitlement-filtered — `getCheckoutOptions` agrees), no `requireVerifiedEmail`, stores `orders.source = 'whatsapp'`.
The browser goes to `/order/<n>?wa=1`; the page builds the message ON THE SERVER from the saved order
(`services/whatsapp-checkout.ts#getWhatsAppHandoff` → `whatsappOrderMessage`, cut to `WHATSAPP_MESSAGE_MAX` keeping totals,
address and order link) and `WhatsAppHandoff` opens `wa.me` once (sessionStorage; `wa=1` removed from the URL), keeps
"Send on WhatsApp" + "Copy order message", and replaces the live timeline/status pill (nobody updates status).
Admin: `WhatsAppOrderBadge` on the orders list + detail. Notifications: `NotificationService.deliver` skips email/push
for `source = 'whatsapp'`. Status stays Pending unless an agent moves it (decision: honest data, no auto-close).
**Test restaurant**: Smash Stop (`npm run db:seed:smash-stop`, slug `smash-stop`, `owner@smashstop.pk` / `SmashStop#1`,
`SEED_WHATSAPP=` to receive orders; borrows Bun Intended's theme/sections/menu/images, own ids, starts with no orders).
Run with `NEXT_PUBLIC_DEFAULT_RESTAURANT=smash-stop`. Verified end to end 2026-10-18 (guest pickup, no email → wa.me
message correct; revisit does not re-open; admin badge; number save/normalise; clearing refused). Tests:
`tests/whatsapp-checkout.test.ts`, `tests/notifications.test.ts` ("WhatsApp checkout orders"). Phase 2 idea: WhatsApp
Business API (orders posted automatically, Confirm/Ready buttons updating status).

**Lifecycle without the API (2026-10-19, migration 0044 `orders.whatsapp_sent_at` + partial index).** wa.me reports
nothing back, so "sent" is the customer's own answer. `shared/whatsapp-order.ts#whatsappStage`: **awaiting** (pending,
unconfirmed, inside `WHATSAPP_SEND_WINDOW_MINUTES` = 30) → **sent** (customer tapped "Yes, I sent it", or staff moved it past
pending) → **not_sent** (window passed, or cancelled with `WHATSAPP_NOT_SENT_REASON` / `WHATSAPP_REPLACED_REASON`) /
**cancelled** (cancelled after sending). Order page (`WhatsAppHandoff`): auto-opens WhatsApp once (sessionStorage
`wa-handoff:<n>`); on coming back (`visibilitychange`/`pageshow`, or a reload after the hand-off) it asks **"Did you press
Send in WhatsApp?"** → `confirmWhatsAppSentAction` (ownership via `findVisitorOrder`) → `confirmWhatsAppSent` →
`repositories/orders.ts#markWhatsAppOrderSent` (guarded update: pending, unconfirmed, inside the window; idempotent; else
`CONFLICT`). Sent view = "Open chat with <restaurant>" (`wa.me/<digits>`) + Call + "Restaurant update: <status>" if staff
moved it; no timeline/ETA/push opt-in. Not-sent view = Reorder. Countdown refreshes at zero. **No scheduler**:
`services/whatsapp-checkout.ts#expireUnsentWhatsAppOrders` → `cancelUnsentWhatsAppOrders` (one UPDATE; cancels stale ones,
and — after a new WhatsApp order — the same phone's older unconfirmed ones) runs lazily before `listOrdersForStaff`,
`getOrderStatusCounts`, `getKitchenOrders`, `getSalesReport`, `getOrdersForExport`, and in `placeOrder`; never throws.
Kitchen also hides awaiting ones (`listKitchenOrders` predicate). Reports exclude them because they become cancelled
(they do show in the Cancelled count, reason "Not sent on WhatsApp"). Admin badge `WhatsAppOrderBadge stage=…`:
"awaiting send" grey / "not sent" red / "WhatsApp" green. Verified end to end 2026-10-19 on Smash Stop (return → ask →
Yes → Sent survives reload; same-phone reorder replaces the first; kitchen hides awaiting; 40-min-old order → not sent
for customer + admin). **Phone layout (fixed 2026-10-19):** `Button` is `whitespace-nowrap`, so a button row indented beside the card's icon overflowed at 390px ("Not yet, open WhatsApp" ran past the card). The card now puts icon + title on one row and the body full width below on phones (`sm:pl-[3.625rem]` indent from sm up), and every action is `w-full sm:w-auto` in a `flex-col sm:flex-row` row. Checked at 390px: no button outside the card, no horizontal page scroll, in all three states.


## 41. Security pass (2026-10-21, migration 0045)

Audit of auth, OTP, reset, admin/super-admin, payments, secrets. **Apply `npm run db:migrate` (0045 `rate_limits`) before deploying**; without it the shared limiter logs a warning and the in-process limits still apply.

### Fixed (each has a test: `tests/security-hardening.test.ts` DB-free, `tests/otp-hardening.test.ts` real Postgres)
- **Guest OTP bypass (confirmed).** `upsertCustomer` changed a guest row's email but kept `is_email_verified`: verify email A, then order/get confirmations sent to ANY email B with no code. Now a different email (case-insensitive) clears the flag. Also `attemptVerificationCode` only counts a code sent to the address the row has NOW (`lower(code.email) = lower(customers.email)`), and `getActiveVerificationCode(customerId, email)` ignores codes for another address so a changed email triggers a new send.
- **Resend reset the guess counter.** Each code had its own 5 attempts, so resends (3 per 5 min) gave unlimited guessing. `attemptVerificationCode(..., maxRecentAttempts=10)` now also locks when the customer's codes of the last 15 min hold ≥ 10 wrong guesses (same statement, so it is atomic). Issuing a code retires earlier ones (`expires_at = now()`, never `consumed_at`: that column means "verified").
- **Guest row claimed as verified.** Sign-up upgrading a guest row (same phone + email) used to inherit `is_email_verified`, so anyone knowing a guest's phone + email got a verified account with their order history. It now stays verified only if that email was OTP-verified in the last 30 min (the checkout "save your details" step), else false (they verify by code like any new account).
- **Account-existence timing.** `verifyPassword` skipped bcrypt for an unknown account (~1 ms vs ~60 ms). It now runs a dummy compare. Applies to staff and customer sign-in.
- **Shared rate limiter.** `rate-limit.ts#checkSharedRateLimit` = in-process bucket first, then an atomic upsert in `rate_limits` (hashed bucket, `repositories/rate-limits.ts`), so limits hold across serverless instances. Used for: per-account staff/customer sign-in, sign-up, code sends (per customer and per IP), password-reset request/verify. Per-IP sign-in stays in-process (CGNAT-generous; avoids a round trip per attempt). Shared store down or table missing → logs and falls back to in-process only (availability of sign-in wins). Code verification needs no extra hop: its cap lives in the verify statement.
- **CSP:** added `object-src 'none'; base-uri 'self'` to the existing `frame-ancestors`. A script-src CSP is NOT set (needs nonces for Next, Stripe, Maps, Firebase); `form-action` is NOT set (JazzCash posts a form to its gateway).
- DB suites that call `createCustomerAccount` now pass a unique identifier (shared limiter rows persist in the test DB between runs).

### Verified OK (do not re-audit)
Every admin Server Action calls `requirePermission`/`requireRestaurantWidePermission`/`requireSuperAdmin` (fresh membership read on writes); a super admin can never be assigned from the app; nobody can grant a role above their own, edit their own role, or reset another login's password via "add staff" (existing logins keep their password); public sign-up never takes a role; Stripe webhook verifies the HMAC + 5 min tolerance over raw bytes; JazzCash return verifies the hash; dispatch route uses a constant-time bearer compare; media route blocks traversal and sandboxes SVG; `safeReturnTo` blocks open redirects; `npm audit --omit=dev` = 0 vulnerabilities; no secrets in tracked files (`repomix-output.xml` is tracked but holds no values).

### Follow-up (same day, migration 0046): three of the remaining risks closed
- **Password reset ends other sessions.** `customers.session_epoch` (0046) is copied into the session JWT (`epoch` claim, absent = 0) and raised by `resetCustomerPassword`; the reset's own new token carries the new value. A session whose epoch is behind is refused where it ACTS on the account: `createOrder` (`CreateOrderInput.sessionEpoch`, compared inside the locked account row), the profile/address service (`requireCustomerId(visitor, restaurantId)`) and My Orders (`getMyOrders`). Page views stay database-free; the header can still show a stale session until its first action. `RequestContext` gained `sessionEpoch` and `customerEmailVerified` (set by `getVisitorContext`); `customer-auth.ts#isSessionCurrent` is the one check. A new sign-in path must put `epoch` in its token (`signInCustomer`, sign-up, Google, reset do).
- **Unverified accounts see no order history or saved addresses** (`getMyOrders` returns empty when the claim says unverified; `getCustomerProfile` returns no addresses until `emailVerified`). Checkout's verify gate is unaffected.
- **`JAZZCASH_ENV=demo` is ignored when `NODE_ENV=production`** unless `ALLOW_PAYMENT_DEMO=true` (then the JazzCash provider is simply not configured). Set the flag only on a public demo site.
- Deploy: `npm run db:migrate` now applies 0045 AND 0046; apply 0046 before the code (the code reads `session_epoch`).

### Remaining risks / manual actions (not fixed in code)
- `callerIdentifier` trusts the first `X-Forwarded-For` hop: correct on Vercel, spoofable behind a proxy that appends; configure the proxy to overwrite it. Per-account limits still apply.
- `images.remotePatterns` allows any https host (the optimizer is an open image proxy); restrict once the image hosts are known.
- No MFA for owner/super-admin logins; staff sessions are 12 h with no server-side revocation list.
- Sign-up says "an account with that email already exists" (account existence is revealed there by design, like the checkout contact check, which is rate limited).

### Open issues parked by the owner (decide later, 2026-10-21)
1. **Image proxy:** `images.remotePatterns` is `https://**`. Known hosts in code: `images.unsplash.com` (seeds), the Supabase storage host (`NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL`); check `website`/menu image URLs in the DB before restricting.
2. **MFA** for owner/super-admin logins.
3. **`X-Forwarded-For`**: have the proxy overwrite it (Vercel already does).
